````js
// ==========================================================
// M13 — writes the graph bank and its structural record (STRUCTURE ONLY; no agent)
// ==========================================================
//   node experiments/m13_generalization/make_bank.mjs [--check]
// Writes graph_bank.json (the 165 modular-ring graphs) and structure.json (descriptors of the bank, the development
// family and the app graph, and the novelty check). With --check it writes nothing and exits 1 unless both files on
// disk are exactly what the generator and the descriptors give.
// ==========================================================
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { makeBank } from './worlds_modular.mjs';
import { structureRecord } from './structure.mjs';

export const N_LABELS = 165;
const HERE = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));

const bank = makeBank(N_LABELS);
const bankText = JSON.stringify(bank, null, 1) + '\n';
const structText = JSON.stringify(structureRecord(bank), null, 1) + '\n';
const bankPath = path.join(HERE, 'graph_bank.json'), structPath = path.join(HERE, 'structure.json');
if (process.argv.includes('--check')) {
  const same = fs.existsSync(bankPath) && fs.readFileSync(bankPath, 'utf8') === bankText && fs.existsSync(structPath) && fs.readFileSync(structPath, 'utf8') === structText;
  console.log(same ? 'graph_bank.json and structure.json are exactly what the generator gives' : 'MISMATCH');
  process.exit(same ? 0 : 1);
}
fs.writeFileSync(bankPath, bankText);
fs.writeFileSync(structPath, structText);
const nv = JSON.parse(structText).novelty;
console.log(`bank: ${bank.count} graphs; novelty ${nv.holds ? 'HOLDS' : 'FAILS'} (bank clustering p05 ${nv.bankClusteringP05.toFixed(3)} vs development p95 ${nv.devClusteringP95.toFixed(3)}; cyclomatic as declared: ${nv.cyclomaticAsDeclared}); skipped events in the bank schedules: ${nv.bank.skippedEvents} of ${nv.bank.events}`);
````

### experiments/m13_generalization/measures.mjs

File: `experiments/m13_generalization/measures.mjs` · SHA-256 (content) `1d9623561943b4f43150b2b14106cc0288c2e98e617ca9f6d6c0422b0ea3e178` · 4364 bytes · 73 lines

````js
// ==========================================================
// M13 — from belief to behaviour: what an agent did on each reopened edge (EVALUATION ONLY)
// ==========================================================
// Input: the environment's own step log (GraphEnv.trace(), runner-only ground truth: [episode, t, node, action, node
// after, reward], one row per step of the whole run), the world, the task's schedule (planE5) and the E5 row. Nothing
// here reaches an agent; it runs after the agent's last step. It works the same for every arm, so MBVI3's behaviour is
// measured exactly as BELIEF's (the gap M12 left).
//
// Event windows are rebuilt from the row: the change phase starts at trace length - row.metric, and each live event
// takes row.events[k].blocked then row.events[k].after steps (the M12 reconstruction).
// For each reversing event (an edge blocked, then reopened):
//   met            the agent tried the edge (either direction) while it was blocked, in the event's blocked window;
//   crossedOwn     moves across the edge in the event's own after-window (what task E5 counts as `crossed`);
//   crossedLater   moves across it after that window, to the end of the run (what M11 did not count);
//   returned       crossedOwn + crossedLater > 0;
//   delay          steps from the reopening to the first move across it (null if none).
// metAndReturned (the G4 count) = reversing events with met and returned.
// verifyAgainstRow checks, for every live event, that the attempts and crossings in the own after-window equal the
// task's own ownAfterAttempts and crossed; the driver throws on any difference.
// ==========================================================
import { edgeKey } from '../../cognitive/testbed/worlds.mjs';

export function windowsOf(row, traceLength, plan) {
  let t = traceLength - row.metric;
  const spans = [];
  plan.events.forEach((e, k) => {
    if (e.skipped) return;
    const ev = row.events[k];
    const blockAt = t, afterAt = t + ev.blocked, endAt = afterAt + ev.after;
    spans.push({ k, key: edgeKey(...e.edge), reverses: e.reverses, blockAt, afterAt, endAt });
    t = endAt;
  });
  if (t !== traceLength) throw new Error(`event windows end at ${t}, the trace has ${traceLength} steps`);
  return spans;
}

export function returnMeasures(world, trace, row, plan) {
  const keyOf = (r) => edgeKey(r[2], world.exitOrder[r[2]][r[3]]);
  const spans = windowsOf(row, trace.length, plan);
  const keys = trace.map(keyOf), moved = trace.map((r) => r[2] !== r[4]);
  const events = spans.map((sp) => {
    let attemptsOwn = 0, crossedOwn = 0, crossedLater = 0, met = false, first = null;
    for (let i = sp.blockAt; i < trace.length; i++) {
      if (keys[i] !== sp.key) continue;
      if (i < sp.afterAt) { met = true; continue; }
      if (i < sp.endAt) { attemptsOwn++; if (moved[i]) crossedOwn++; } else if (moved[i]) crossedLater++;
      if (moved[i] && first === null) first = i;
    }
    return { k: sp.k, reverses: sp.reverses, met, attemptsOwn, crossedOwn, crossedLater, returned: sp.reverses && crossedOwn + crossedLater > 0, delay: sp.reverses && first !== null ? first - sp.afterAt : null };
  });
  const rev = events.filter((e) => e.reverses);
  return {
    events,
    reversing: rev.length,
    met: rev.filter((e) => e.met).length,
    metAndReturned: rev.filter((e) => e.met && e.returned).length,
    returnedOwn: rev.filter((e) => e.crossedOwn > 0).length,
    returnedOnlyLater: rev.filter((e) => e.crossedOwn === 0 && e.crossedLater > 0).length,
    crossingsOwn: rev.reduce((s, e) => s + e.crossedOwn, 0),
    crossingsLater: rev.reduce((s, e) => s + e.crossedLater, 0),
    delays: rev.filter((e) => e.met && e.delay !== null).map((e) => e.delay),
  };
}

/** Throws unless the own-window counts agree with the task's own diagnostics for every live event. */
export function verifyAgainstRow(measures, row) {
  for (const e of measures.events) {
    const ev = row.events[e.k];
    if (ev.ownAfterAttempts !== e.attemptsOwn) throw new Error(`${row.world} ${row.family} event ${e.k}: ${e.attemptsOwn} own-window attempts, the task counted ${ev.ownAfterAttempts}`);
    if (e.reverses && ev.crossed !== e.crossedOwn) throw new Error(`${row.world} ${row.family} event ${e.k}: ${e.crossedOwn} own-window crossings, the task counted ${ev.crossed}`);
  }
  return true;
}
````

### experiments/m13_generalization/m13_lib.mjs

File: `experiments/m13_generalization/m13_lib.mjs` · SHA-256 (content) `0eb35fd1eecb8f05bb3dd870dfc57d30e68bfa6151c89bc393c5f69ec7617a3c` · 17120 bytes · 236 lines

````js
// ==========================================================
// M13 — cognitive generalization: configuration, arms, one run, analysis and reading rule
// ==========================================================
// The question: does the adopted AD-13 belief keep its advantage over MBVI3 in a held-out family of worlds that is
// structurally different from the one it was developed on, with nothing tuned? Everything the agents and the task use
// already exists and is used unchanged:
//   - task E5 (cognitive/testbed/task_e5.mjs) with its frozen parameters;
//   - BELIEF = makeAdoptedAgent (AD-13); MBVI3 = the registry's MBVI3; BELIEF_REALONLY / BELIEF_FALSEONLY = CF-06's
//     privileged diagnostic arms (arms_v6.mjs), secondary and in no criterion.
// New here: the modular-ring worlds (worlds_modular.mjs), the structural record (structure.mjs), the behaviour
// measures for every arm (measures.mjs), and the analysis and reading rule below (SPEC.md).
// Nothing in the browser application (main.js, render/, index.html) is read, imported or changed.
// ==========================================================
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { generateWorld } from '../../cognitive/testbed/worlds.mjs';
import { runE5, planE5, E5_FAMILIES, E5_PARAMS } from '../../cognitive/testbed/task_e5.mjs';
import { ADOPTED_AGENT, makeAdoptedAgent } from '../../cognitive/testbed/adopted_agent.mjs';
import { makeAgentForV6 } from '../../cognitive/testbed/registry_v6.mjs';
import { armAgentV6 } from '../../cognitive/testbed/arms_v6.mjs';
import { pairedBootstrap } from '../../cognitive/testbed/stats.mjs';
import { traced } from '../m11_routes/trace.mjs';
import { buildWorld, BANK_LABEL } from './worlds_modular.mjs';
import { returnMeasures, verifyAgainstRow } from './measures.mjs';

export const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..', '..');
export const N_LABELS = 165;
export const FAMILIES = Object.freeze(['RARE', 'HALF', 'OFTEN']);
export const TRACE_LABELS = Object.freeze([0, 1, 2]);
export const MARGIN = 25;                                      // G2: CF-06's B2 margin, unchanged
export const REFERENCE_EFFECTS = Object.freeze({ CF06_gate6: 0.444, M11_app_graph: 0.498 });   // G1's unit, mean / SD (proposal_inputs/power_inputs.out)
export const LABEL_OF = Object.freeze({ m13: BANK_LABEL, smoke: (i) => `m13smoke|dev|${i}` });
// Label namespaces already used by any earlier stage, test, survey or smoke run; no M13 world label may start with one.
export const BARRED_PREFIXES = Object.freeze(['cf|', 'mfw|', 'm13survey|', 'm13test|', 'm13smoke|', 'm13|structure|']);

export function assertFreshLabels(labels) {
  for (const l of labels) {
    if (!l.startsWith('m13|modular|')) throw new Error(`label ${l} is not in the M13 namespace m13|modular|*`);
    if (BARRED_PREFIXES.some((p) => l.startsWith(p))) throw new Error(`label ${l} is in a namespace already used`);
  }
  if (new Set(labels).size !== labels.length) throw new Error('duplicate labels');
  return true;
}

/** The arms. BELIEF and the two diagnostic arms carry exactly the adopted agent's parameters. */
const beliefDiagnostic = (diagnostic) => (env, label, context) => armAgentV6([ADOPTED_AGENT.kind, { ...ADOPTED_AGENT.params }, { diagnostic }])(ADOPTED_AGENT.kind, env, label, {}, context);
export const ARMS = Object.freeze({
  BELIEF: Object.freeze({ kind: ADOPTED_AGENT.kind, role: 'primary (AD-13)', make: (env, label) => makeAdoptedAgent(env, label) }),
  MBVI3: Object.freeze({ kind: 'MBVI3', role: 'baseline', make: (env, label) => makeAgentForV6('MBVI3', env, label, {}) }),
  BELIEF_REALONLY: Object.freeze({ kind: ADOPTED_AGENT.kind, role: 'secondary, privileged: archives genuine changes only', make: beliefDiagnostic('real') }),
  BELIEF_FALSEONLY: Object.freeze({ kind: ADOPTED_AGENT.kind, role: 'secondary, privileged: archives false alarms only', make: beliefDiagnostic('false') }),
});
export const CORE = Object.freeze(['BELIEF', 'MBVI3']);

/** The worlds of a domain: the frozen bank for m13, the development generator for smoke runs. */
export function worldsOf(domain, bank, count) {
  if (domain === 'm13') return bank.graphs.map((g) => [g.label, buildWorld(g.label, g.n, g.edges)]);
  return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, i) => { const l = LABEL_OF.smoke(i); return [l, generateWorld(l)]; });
}

/** One E5 run of one arm. The environment's own step log gives the behaviour measures; withTrace adds the recorder. */
export function runOne(world, worldLabel, family, arm, withTrace) {
  let env = null, rec = null;
  const makeAgent = (_kind, e, label, _params, context) => {
    env = e;
    const agent = ARMS[arm].make(e, label, context);
    if (!withTrace) return agent;
    rec = traced(agent, context);
    return rec.agent;
  };
  const row = runE5({ world, worldLabel, family, kind: ARMS[arm].kind, makeAgent });
  if (row.excluded) return { row: { arm, ...row }, record: null };
  const measures = returnMeasures(world, env.trace(), row, planE5(world, worldLabel, family));
  verifyAgainstRow(measures, row);
  return { row: { arm, ...row, m13: measures }, record: rec ? rec.record() : null };
}

/** A recorder's output with internal node ids (the modular worlds have no app ids). */
export function readableTrace(world, record, row, worldLabel, family) {
  const edgeOf = (label, a) => { const u = world.nodeOfLabel.get(label); return [u, world.exitOrder[u][a]]; };
  const plan = planE5(world, worldLabel, family);
  const log = row.secondary?.volatility?.log ?? null;
  return {
    world: worldLabel, family, arm: row.arm, start: plan.start, goal: plan.goal,
    schedule: plan.events.map((e) => (e.skipped ? { skipped: true } : { edge: e.edge, reverses: e.reverses })),
    stepColumns: ['t', 'from', 'to', 'moved', 'blocked', 'pWorks'],
    steps: record.steps.map(([t, s, a, m, b, p]) => [t, ...edgeOf(s, a), m, b, p]),
    followedColumns: ['t', 'pWorks', 'blocked', 'tried'],
    followed: record.followed.map((f) => { const [from, to] = edgeOf(f.node, f.action); return { from, to, points: f.points }; }),
    beliefEvents: log ? log.map(([type, label, a, t, rho]) => ({ type, t, edge: edgeOf(label, a), rho })) : null,
  };
}

// ---- analysis ----
const sum = (xs) => xs.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
const mean = (xs) => (xs.length ? sum(xs) / xs.length : null);
const sd = (xs) => { if (xs.length < 2) return null; const m = mean(xs); return Math.sqrt(xs.reduce((s, x) => s + (x - m) ** 2, 0) / (xs.length - 1)); };
const median = (xs) => { if (!xs.length) return null; const s = [...xs].sort((a, b) => a - b), n = s.length; return n % 2 ? s[(n - 1) / 2] : (s[n / 2 - 1] + s[n / 2]) / 2; };

export const MEASURES = Object.freeze({
  total: (r) => r.metric, blocked: (r) => r.windows.blocked, afterReversed: (r) => r.windows.afterReversed, afterPermanent: (r) => r.windows.afterPermanent,
  futileRetests: (r) => r.diagnostics.futileRetests, censored: (r) => r.censored,
  met: (r) => r.m13.met, metAndReturned: (r) => r.m13.metAndReturned, returnedOwn: (r) => r.m13.returnedOwn, returnedOnlyLater: (r) => r.m13.returnedOnlyLater, crossingsLater: (r) => r.m13.crossingsLater,
});
// [name, text, measure, sign]: steps as MBVI3 - BELIEF (positive = the belief takes fewer); cost and behaviour counts as
// BELIEF - MBVI3.
const CONTRASTS = Object.freeze([
  ['total', 'MBVI3 - BELIEF', 'total', -1], ['cost', 'BELIEF - MBVI3', 'total', 1], ['blocked', 'MBVI3 - BELIEF', 'blocked', -1],
  ['afterReversed', 'MBVI3 - BELIEF', 'afterReversed', -1], ['afterPermanent', 'MBVI3 - BELIEF', 'afterPermanent', -1],
  ['futileRetests', 'BELIEF - MBVI3', 'futileRetests', 1], ['metAndReturned', 'BELIEF - MBVI3', 'metAndReturned', 1],
  ['returnedOwn', 'BELIEF - MBVI3', 'returnedOwn', 1], ['returnedOnlyLater', 'BELIEF - MBVI3', 'returnedOnlyLater', 1],
]);

function contrastOf(d, text, label) {
  return { contrast: text, sd: sd(d), median: median(d), positive: d.filter((x) => x > 0).length, negative: d.filter((x) => x < 0).length, zero: d.filter((x) => x === 0).length, ...pairedBootstrap(d, label) };
}
/** CF-06's gainShape test for one criterion: more labels in favour than against, a positive median, and a positive mean once the most favourable ceil(0.1 n) labels are removed. */
function shapeOf(d) {
  const s = [...d].sort((a, b) => b - a), top = Math.ceil(0.1 * d.length), rest = s.slice(top);
  const why = [];
  const inFavour = d.filter((x) => x > 0).length, against = d.filter((x) => x < 0).length;
  if (!(inFavour > against)) why.push(`${inFavour} of ${d.length} labels in favour, ${against} against`);
  if (!(median(d) > 0)) why.push(`median ${median(d)}`);
  if (!(mean(rest) > 0)) why.push(`no gain without the top ${top} labels`);
  return { inFavour, against, ties: d.length - inFavour - against, median: median(d), topShare: sum(d) ? sum(s.slice(0, top)) / sum(d) : null, meanWithoutTop: mean(rest), fails: why };
}

/** excess: Map `${label}|${family}` -> per-event detour excess (structure.mjs detourExcess). */
export function analyse(rows, domain, excess) {
  const tag = (name) => `m13|stats|${domain}|${name}`;
  const cell = (family, arm) => new Map(rows.filter((r) => r.family === family && r.arm === arm && !r.excluded).map((r) => [r.world, r]));
  const out = { excludedRuns: rows.filter((r) => r.excluded && r.arm === 'BELIEF').length, families: {}, criteria: {}, secondary: {} };
  const pairs = (family, A, Bn, f, sign = -1) => { const X = cell(family, A), Y = cell(family, Bn); const labels = [...X.keys()].filter((w) => Y.has(w)).sort(); return labels.map((w) => (sign === -1 ? f(Y.get(w)) - f(X.get(w)) : f(X.get(w)) - f(Y.get(w)))); };
  for (const family of FAMILIES) {
    const B = cell(family, 'BELIEF'), M = cell(family, 'MBVI3');
    const labels = [...B.keys()].filter((w) => M.has(w)).sort();
    const arms = {};
    for (const arm of Object.keys(ARMS)) {
      const c = cell(family, arm), ls = labels.filter((w) => c.has(w));
      arms[arm] = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(MEASURES).map(([k, f]) => [k, mean(ls.map((w) => f(c.get(w))))]));
      arms[arm].skippedEvents = ls.reduce((s, w) => s + c.get(w).skippedEvents, 0);
      arms[arm].firstReturnDelayMedian = median(ls.flatMap((w) => c.get(w).m13.delays));
      arms[arm].detector = { changesDown: mean(ls.map((w) => c.get(w).secondary.model.changesDown)), changesUp: mean(ls.map((w) => c.get(w).secondary.model.changesUp)) };
    }
    const contrasts = {};
    for (const [name, text, measure, sign] of CONTRASTS) {
      const f = MEASURES[measure];
      contrasts[name] = contrastOf(labels.map((w) => sign * (f(B.get(w)) - f(M.get(w)))), `${text}, ${measure} (${family})`, tag(`${family}|${name}`));
    }
    const alarms = labels.map((w) => B.get(w).diagnostics.alarms);
    const belief = Object.fromEntries(['opensReal', 'opensRealReversing', 'opensRealPermanent', 'opensFalse', 'confirmsReal', 'confirmsFalse'].map((k) => [k, mean(alarms.map((a) => (a ? a[k] : 0)))]));
    out.families[family] = { labels: labels.length, arms, contrasts, beliefArchivesPerRun: belief };
  }
  // ---- criteria ----
  const fams = FAMILIES.map((f) => [cell(f, 'BELIEF'), cell(f, 'MBVI3')]);
  const mixLabels = [...fams[0][0].keys()].filter((w) => fams.every(([B, M]) => B.has(w) && M.has(w))).sort();
  const g1 = mixLabels.map((w) => fams.reduce((s, [B, M]) => s + (M.get(w).metric - B.get(w).metric), 0));
  const g3 = pairs('OFTEN', 'BELIEF', 'MBVI3', MEASURES.afterReversed);
  out.criteria = {
    G1_mix: contrastOf(g1, 'per label, sum over RARE + HALF + OFTEN of steps(MBVI3) - steps(BELIEF)', tag('G1|mix')),
    G2_RARE_cost: out.families.RARE.contrasts.cost,
    G3_OFTEN_afterReversed: contrastOf(g3, 'OFTEN, steps after reopenings, MBVI3 - BELIEF', tag('G3|OFTEN|afterReversed')),
    G4_OFTEN_metAndReturned: out.families.OFTEN.contrasts.metAndReturned,
  };
  out.gainShape = { G1_mix: shapeOf(g1), G3_OFTEN_afterReversed: shapeOf(g3) };
  // ---- secondary (never in a criterion) ----
  const g1sd = sd(g1);
  out.secondary.standardizedEffectG1 = { value: g1sd ? mean(g1) / g1sd : null, reference: REFERENCE_EFFECTS, note: 'mean / SD of the per-label G1 differences, beside the same quantity in CF-06 gate6 and M11' };
  const real = (f, m) => pairs(f, 'BELIEF_REALONLY', 'MBVI3', m), fals = (f, m) => pairs(f, 'BELIEF_FALSEONLY', 'MBVI3', m);
  const mixOf = (arm) => mixLabels.map((w) => FAMILIES.reduce((s, f) => s + (cell(f, 'MBVI3').get(w).metric - cell(f, arm).get(w).metric), 0));
  const exact = (name, kind) => mixLabels.every((w) => FAMILIES.every((f) => cell(f, name).get(w).diagnostics.alarms[kind] === 0));
  const AT = {
    splitExact: exact('BELIEF_REALONLY', 'opensFalse') && exact('BELIEF_FALSEONLY', 'opensReal'),
    OFTEN: {
      genuineChanges: contrastOf(real('OFTEN', MEASURES.total), 'MBVI3 - BELIEF_REALONLY (OFTEN)', tag('attr|OFTEN|real')),
      genuineAfterReversed: contrastOf(real('OFTEN', MEASURES.afterReversed), 'MBVI3 - BELIEF_REALONLY, after reopenings (OFTEN)', tag('attr|OFTEN|realAfter')),
      falseAlarms: contrastOf(fals('OFTEN', MEASURES.total), 'MBVI3 - BELIEF_FALSEONLY (OFTEN)', tag('attr|OFTEN|false')),
    },
    mix: { genuineChanges: contrastOf(mixOf('BELIEF_REALONLY'), 'MBVI3 - BELIEF_REALONLY, mix', tag('attr|mix|real')), falseAlarms: contrastOf(mixOf('BELIEF_FALSEONLY'), 'MBVI3 - BELIEF_FALSEONLY, mix', tag('attr|mix|false')) },
  };
  const lb = (c) => !!c && c.n > 0 && c.mean > 0 && c.lo > 0;
  const source = (genuine, falseRepair) => (!AT.splitExact ? 'INVALID (the diagnostic split is not exact)' : genuine && falseRepair ? 'GENUINE REVERSALS AND FALSE-ALARM REPAIR' : genuine ? 'GENUINE REVERSALS' : falseRepair ? 'FALSE-ALARM REPAIR' : 'NOT ATTRIBUTED');
  AT.gainSource = source(lb(AT.OFTEN.genuineChanges) && lb(AT.OFTEN.genuineAfterReversed), lb(AT.OFTEN.falseAlarms));
  AT.gainSourceMix = source(lb(AT.mix.genuineChanges), lb(AT.mix.falseAlarms));
  out.secondary.attribution = AT;
  // moderator: per reversing event, the after-window difference MBVI3 - BELIEF by the event's detour excess
  const mod = {};
  for (const family of FAMILIES) {
    const B = cell(family, 'BELIEF'), M = cell(family, 'MBVI3'), groups = { 'excess 0': [], 'excess 1-3': [], 'excess 4+': [] };
    for (const w of [...B.keys()].filter((x) => M.has(x))) {
      const ex = excess.get(`${w}|${family}`);
      B.get(w).events.forEach((e, k) => {
        if (e.skipped || !e.reverses) return;
        const x = ex[k], g = x === 0 ? 'excess 0' : x >= 4 ? 'excess 4+' : 'excess 1-3';
        groups[g].push(M.get(w).events[k].after - e.after);
      });
    }
    mod[family] = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(groups).map(([g, d]) => [g, { events: d.length, meanAfterDifference: mean(d) }]));
  }
  out.secondary.moderatorDetourExcess = { note: 'descriptive: per reversing event, steps in its after-window, MBVI3 - BELIEF, by the detour excess of its block', ...mod };
  return out;
}

// ---- reading rule (SPEC.md); strict inequalities; an empty contrast never passes ----
const lbPos = (c) => !!c && c.n > 0 && c.mean > 0 && c.lo > 0;
const ubBelow = (c, x) => !!c && c.n > 0 && c.hi < x;

/**
 * SPEC.md "Verdict": five independent lines (one per criterion) and one summary statement from G0, G1 and G2 only.
 * validity: { identical, testsPassed, novelty, noExclusions } (G0).
 */
export function readingRule(analysis, validity) {
  const C = analysis.criteria;
  const g0parts = { determinism: !!validity.identical, tests: !!validity.testsPassed, novelty: !!validity.novelty, exclusions: !!validity.noExclusions };
  const valid = Object.values(g0parts).every(Boolean);
  const g1 = C.G1_mix;
  const lines = {
    validity: valid ? 'VALID' : `INVALID (${Object.entries(g0parts).filter(([, v]) => !v).map(([k]) => k).join(', ')})`,
    netAdvantage: lbPos(g1) ? 'RETAINED' : !!g1 && g1.n > 0 && g1.hi < 0 ? 'REVERSED' : 'NOT SHOWN',
    safety: ubBelow(C.G2_RARE_cost, MARGIN) ? 'SAFE' : 'NOT SHOWN SAFE',
    recovery: lbPos(C.G3_OFTEN_afterReversed) ? 'SHOWN' : 'NOT SHOWN',
    behaviouralMechanism: lbPos(C.G4_OFTEN_metAndReturned) ? 'SHOWN' : 'NOT SHOWN',
  };
  const shape = (k) => (analysis.gainShape[k].fails.length ? `TAIL-DRIVEN (${analysis.gainShape[k].fails.join(', ')})` : 'CONSISTENT');
  const gainShape = { G1_mix: shape('G1_mix'), G3_OFTEN_afterReversed: shape('G3_OFTEN_afterReversed') };
  let summary;
  if (!valid) summary = 'INVALID — NO CRITERION IS INTERPRETED';
  else if (lines.netAdvantage === 'RETAINED') summary = `NET ADVANTAGE RETAINED ON THE MODULAR FAMILY${lines.safety === 'SAFE' ? ', WITHIN THE SAFETY MARGIN' : '; SAFETY NOT SHOWN'}${gainShape.G1_mix === 'CONSISTENT' ? '' : ' — FROM A MINORITY OF LABELS'}`;
  else summary = `NET ADVANTAGE ${lines.netAdvantage} ON THE MODULAR FAMILY`;
  return {
    G0_validity: valid, G1_net: lines.netAdvantage === 'RETAINED', G2_safety: lines.safety === 'SAFE', G3_recovery: lines.recovery === 'SHOWN', G4_behaviour: lines.behaviouralMechanism === 'SHOWN',
    g0parts, lines, gainShape, summary,
  };
}

export { E5_FAMILIES, E5_PARAMS };
````

### experiments/m13_generalization/pins.mjs

File: `experiments/m13_generalization/pins.mjs` · SHA-256 (content) `4a00ba98585660ad90754c0d7e83ce57696c1e499faf319c2e2c9e4788235227` · 4066 bytes · 70 lines

````js
// ==========================================================
// M13 — pins: the SHA-256 of every file the run executes, found by following its static imports
// ==========================================================
//   node experiments/m13_generalization/pins.mjs           writes PINS.json
//   node experiments/m13_generalization/pins.mjs --check   exits 1 unless PINS.json matches the files on disk
// The closure starts at run_m13.mjs and follows every relative `import ... from '...'` / `export ... from '...'`
// (node: built-ins are not files of the repository). The agents' code (cognitive/mind, cognitive/agents,
// cognitive/testbed), task E5, the recorder and M13's own modules are all in it. The graph bank and the structural
// record are pinned beside them. The driver refuses to run unless the closure and every hash equal PINS.json.
// ==========================================================
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const HERE = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const ROOT = path.resolve(HERE, '..', '..');
// Content hash with CRLF read as LF: the agents' code has no exact-bytes rule, so a checkout with core.autocrlf=true
// writes CRLF. That conversion is not a change of the code; any change of its content is.
const sha = (b) => crypto.createHash('sha256').update(Buffer.from(b.toString('utf8').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n'), 'utf8')).digest('hex');
const rel = (p) => path.relative(ROOT, p).split(path.sep).join('/');
const IMPORT = /(?:^|[\s;])(?:import|export)\s[^'"`]*?from\s*['"]([^'"]+)['"]|(?:^|[\s;])import\s*['"]([^'"]+)['"]/gm;
export const ENTRY = path.join(HERE, 'run_m13.mjs');
// Pinned beside the closure: the frozen data, and the two scripts the driver runs as child processes (not imports).
export const DATA = Object.freeze(['experiments/m13_generalization/graph_bank.json', 'experiments/m13_generalization/structure.json', 'experiments/m13_generalization/make_bank.mjs', 'scripts/test.mjs']);

export function closure(entry = ENTRY) {
  const seen = new Set(), stack = [entry];
  while (stack.length) {
    const f = stack.pop();
    if (seen.has(f)) continue;
    seen.add(f);
    const text = fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8');
    for (const m of text.matchAll(IMPORT)) {
      const spec = m[1] ?? m[2];
      if (!spec.startsWith('.')) continue;
      stack.push(path.resolve(path.dirname(f), spec));
    }
  }
  return [...seen].map(rel).sort();
}

export function pinsNow() {
  const files = [...closure(), ...DATA].sort();
  return Object.fromEntries(files.map((f) => [f, sha(fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, f)))]));
}

/** Differences between PINS.json and the files on disk ([] when they agree). */
export function pinDifferences() {
  const file = path.join(HERE, 'PINS.json');
  if (!fs.existsSync(file)) return ['PINS.json is missing'];
  const frozen = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8')).files, now = pinsNow(), diff = [];
  for (const f of new Set([...Object.keys(frozen), ...Object.keys(now)])) {
    if (!(f in now)) diff.push(`${f}: pinned but no longer imported`);
    else if (!(f in frozen)) diff.push(`${f}: imported but not pinned`);
    else if (frozen[f] !== now[f]) diff.push(`${f}: hash ${now[f].slice(0, 12)} differs from the pinned ${frozen[f].slice(0, 12)}`);
  }
  return diff;
}

if (process.argv[1] && path.resolve(process.argv[1]) === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) {
  if (process.argv.includes('--check')) {
    const d = pinDifferences();
    console.log(d.length ? `PINS MISMATCH:\n${d.join('\n')}` : 'PINS.json matches every pinned file');
    process.exit(d.length ? 1 : 0);
  }
  const files = pinsNow();
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(HERE, 'PINS.json'), JSON.stringify({ note: 'SHA-256 (content, CRLF read as LF) of every file run_m13.mjs executes (static import closure), of the frozen data and of the two scripts it runs; checked by the driver before the run', files }, null, 1) + '\n');
  console.log(`PINS.json: ${Object.keys(files).length} files`);
}
````

File: `experiments/m13_generalization/run_m13.mjs` · SHA-256 (content) `4ed2abdefb1e553ab7a4dddfc8f84d9bac9b46ac4e125ae9b8ac28faea67d14e` · 9650 bytes · 134 lines

````js
// ==========================================================
// M13 driver — cognitive generalization: the AD-13 belief against MBVI3 on the held-out modular-ring family
// ==========================================================
//   node experiments/m13_generalization/run_m13.mjs --domain m13
//       The one run. It refuses unless:
//         - SPEC.md is marked Frozen and every line of SPEC.sha256 matches (SPEC.md, PINS.json, the bank, the record);
//         - every file the run executes matches PINS.json (pins.mjs);
//         - cognitive/, scripts/, experiments/m13_generalization/ and the recorder are committed and clean;
//         - the graph bank is exactly what the generator gives, its labels are fresh, and the novelty check holds;
//         - the whole test suite passes (node scripts/test.mjs);
//         - no result file exists.
//   node experiments/m13_generalization/run_m13.mjs --domain smoke --labels 1 --out <file outside the repository>
//       A smoke run on development-family worlds (labels m13smoke|dev|*). It never touches the modular family. It
//       prints no measure and no verdict; it exists to show that the driver runs end to end, and how long a run takes.
//
// Run A runs all four arms and records traces for the core arms on TRACE_LABELS. Run B repeats the core arms without
// the recorder. The core rows of the two runs must be identical (G0). The result is written only after both runs.
// ==========================================================
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import { execFileSync, spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { performance } from 'node:perf_hooks';
import {
  ROOT, N_LABELS, LABEL_OF, FAMILIES, TRACE_LABELS, MARGIN, ARMS, CORE, E5_FAMILIES, E5_PARAMS,
  assertFreshLabels, worldsOf, runOne, readableTrace, analyse, readingRule,
} from './m13_lib.mjs';
import { ADOPTED_AGENT } from '../../cognitive/testbed/adopted_agent.mjs';
import { checkBank, MODULAR } from './worlds_modular.mjs';
import { detourExcess } from './structure.mjs';
import { pinDifferences } from './pins.mjs';

const HERE = path.join(ROOT, 'experiments', 'm13_generalization');
const sha = (b) => crypto.createHash('sha256').update(b).digest('hex');
const arg = (n, d = null) => { const i = process.argv.indexOf(n); return i > 0 ? process.argv[i + 1] : d; };
const die = (m) => { console.error(`m13 run_m13 refused: ${m}`); process.exit(2); };
const git = (...a) => execFileSync('git', ['-C', ROOT, ...a]).toString().trim();

const DOMAIN = arg('--domain');
if (!['smoke', 'm13'].includes(DOMAIN)) die('--domain must be smoke or m13');
const isRun = DOMAIN === 'm13';
const SPEC = path.join(HERE, 'SPEC.md');
const OUT = isRun ? path.join(HERE, 'results', 'm13_result.json') : path.resolve(arg('--out') ?? '');
const headAtLaunch = git('rev-parse', 'HEAD');
let bank = null, structure = null, tests = null;

if (!isRun) {
  const relOut = path.relative(ROOT, OUT);
  if (!arg('--out') || (!relOut.startsWith('..') && !path.isAbsolute(relOut))) die('smoke runs need --out outside the repository');
} else {
  if (!fs.existsSync(SPEC)) die('SPEC.md is missing');
  if (!/^\*\*Status\.\*\* Frozen/m.test(fs.readFileSync(SPEC, 'utf8'))) die('SPEC.md is not marked Frozen (it is still a draft)');
  const SHAFILE = path.join(HERE, 'SPEC.sha256');
  if (!fs.existsSync(SHAFILE)) die('SPEC.sha256 (the frozen hashes) is missing');
  const lines = fs.readFileSync(SHAFILE, 'utf8').trim().split(/\r?\n/).map((l) => l.trim().split(/\s+/));
  const need = ['SPEC.md', 'PINS.json', 'graph_bank.json', 'structure.json'];
  for (const f of need) if (!lines.some(([, name]) => name === f)) die(`SPEC.sha256 has no line for ${f}`);
  for (const [h, name] of lines) if (sha(fs.readFileSync(path.join(HERE, name))) !== h) die(`${name} does not match its frozen SHA-256 ${h}`);
  const pins = pinDifferences();
  if (pins.length) die(`files differ from PINS.json:\n${pins.join('\n')}`);
  const dirty = git('status', '--porcelain', '--untracked-files=all', '--', 'cognitive', 'scripts', 'experiments/m13_generalization', 'experiments/m11_routes/trace.mjs');
  if (dirty) die(`files are not all committed and clean:\n${dirty}`);
  if (fs.existsSync(OUT)) die('the M13 labels have already been run');
  bank = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(HERE, 'graph_bank.json'), 'utf8'));
  try { checkBank(bank); assertFreshLabels(bank.graphs.map((g) => g.label)); } catch (e) { die(e.message); }
  if (bank.count !== N_LABELS) die(`the bank has ${bank.count} graphs, the specification ${N_LABELS}`);
  structure = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(HERE, 'structure.json'), 'utf8'));
  const check = spawnSync(process.execPath, [path.join(HERE, 'make_bank.mjs'), '--check'], { cwd: ROOT, encoding: 'utf8' });
  if (check.status !== 0) die('structure.json or graph_bank.json is not exactly what the generator and the descriptors give');
  if (!structure.novelty.holds) die('the novelty check does not hold: the bank is not structurally outside the development family');
  const t = spawnSync(process.execPath, [path.join(ROOT, 'scripts', 'test.mjs')], { cwd: ROOT, encoding: 'utf8', maxBuffer: 1 << 26 });
  const total = (t.stdout.match(/TOTAL (\d+)\/(\d+)/) ?? []).slice(1).map(Number);
  tests = { exit: t.status, passed: total[0] ?? null, total: total[1] ?? null };
  if (t.status !== 0) die(`the test suite does not pass (${t.stdout.trim().split('\n').pop()})`);
}

const N = isRun ? N_LABELS : Number(arg('--labels', '1'));
if (!(Number.isInteger(N) && N > 0)) die('--labels must be a positive integer');
const worlds = worldsOf(DOMAIN, bank, N);
const excess = new Map(worlds.flatMap(([label, w]) => FAMILIES.map((f) => [`${label}|${f}`, detourExcess(w, label, f)])));
const appFiles = Object.fromEntries(['main.js', 'index.html', 'connections.json', 'neurons.json'].map((f) => [f, sha(fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, f)))]));

const t0 = performance.now();
function runAll(arms, withTraces, timing, tag) {
  const rows = [], traces = [];
  worlds.forEach(([worldLabel, world], i) => {
    for (const family of FAMILIES) for (const arm of arms) {
      const traceThis = withTraces && CORE.includes(arm) && TRACE_LABELS.includes(i);
      const s = performance.now();
      const { row, record } = runOne(world, worldLabel, family, arm, traceThis);
      timing[arm] = (timing[arm] ?? 0) + performance.now() - s;
      rows.push(row);
      if (record) traces.push(readableTrace(world, record, row, worldLabel, family));
    }
    console.log(`${tag}: label ${i + 1} of ${worlds.length} done, ${Math.round((performance.now() - t0) / 1000)} s`);
  });
  return { rows, traces };
}
const timingA = {}, timingB = {};
const A = runAll(Object.keys(ARMS), true, timingA, 'run A');
const B = runAll(CORE, false, timingB, 'run B');
const coreA = A.rows.filter((r) => CORE.includes(r.arm));
const rowsShaA = sha(JSON.stringify(coreA)), rowsShaB = sha(JSON.stringify(B.rows));
const analysis = analyse(A.rows, DOMAIN, excess);
const excludedPairs = A.rows.filter((r) => r.arm === 'BELIEF' && r.excluded).length;
const validity = { identical: rowsShaA === rowsShaB, testsPassed: isRun ? tests.exit === 0 : null, novelty: isRun ? structure.novelty.holds : null, noExclusions: excludedPairs === 0, excludedPairs };
const reading = readingRule(analysis, validity);
const headAtEnd = git('rev-parse', 'HEAD');

const result = {
  evidence: 'M13 cognitive generalization on a held-out modular-ring family: one run, by the designer, on local commits; development-level, not confirmatory',
  domain: DOMAIN, labels: N, labelPattern: LABEL_OF[DOMAIN]('<i>'),
  worlds: isRun ? { family: bank.family, generator: MODULAR, bankSha256: sha(fs.readFileSync(path.join(HERE, 'graph_bank.json'))) } : { family: 'development generator (smoke)', generator: 'generateWorld defaults' },
  structure: isRun ? structure : null,
  specSha256: isRun ? sha(fs.readFileSync(SPEC)) : null,
  pinsSha256: isRun ? sha(fs.readFileSync(path.join(HERE, 'PINS.json'))) : null,
  headAtLaunch, headAtEnd,
  environment: { node: process.version, platform: `${process.platform}-${process.arch}` },
  appFiles, tests,
  parameters: {
    arms: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(ARMS).map(([k, a]) => [k, { kind: a.kind, role: a.role, params: k === 'MBVI3' ? {} : { ...ADOPTED_AGENT.params } }])),
    adoptedAgent: { kind: ADOPTED_AGENT.kind, params: { ...ADOPTED_AGENT.params }, decision: ADOPTED_AGENT.decision },
    task: E5_PARAMS, families: E5_FAMILIES, traceLabels: TRACE_LABELS, margin: MARGIN,
  },
  determinism: { rowsSha256RunA: rowsShaA, rowsSha256RunB: rowsShaB, identical: rowsShaA === rowsShaB, note: 'core arms: run A (traced on the trace labels) against run B (untraced)' },
  resources: { note: 'not part of the hashed rows', wallClockMsRunA: timingA, wallClockMsRunB: timingB, totalSeconds: Math.round((performance.now() - t0) / 1000), peakRssKb: Math.round(process.resourceUsage().maxRSS) },
  validity, analysis, reading,
  traces: A.traces,
  rows: A.rows,
};
fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(OUT), { recursive: true });
fs.writeFileSync(OUT, JSON.stringify(result, null, 1) + '\n');
if (isRun) console.log(`M13 m13: ${reading.summary}; core runs identical: ${validity.identical}; ${result.resources.totalSeconds} s; ${OUT}`);
else console.log(`M13 smoke: ${N} development-family labels, ${A.rows.length} + ${B.rows.length} rows; core runs identical: ${validity.identical}; seconds per run: ${Object.entries(timingA).map(([k, v]) => `${k} ${(v / 1000 / (N * FAMILIES.length)).toFixed(2)}`).join(', ')}; ${result.resources.totalSeconds} s; ${OUT} (no measure printed)`);
````

### cognitive/agents/dynaq.mjs

File: `cognitive/agents/dynaq.mjs` · SHA-256 (content) `5a953fc7dbabcbb4f8d6150726204d69534067186145627bc42c25455f1ed01f` · 2496 bytes · 49 lines

````js
// ==========================================================
// DYNAQ — QL plus classic tabular Dyna-Q planning (model-based positive control; Sutton & Barto 2018 §8.2)
// ==========================================================
// Model: for every experienced (node, action) the last observed (next node, its exit count, reward, done).
// After every real update, k planning updates on (node, action) pairs drawn uniformly from the experienced pairs
// (in first-experience order).  Planning targets the CURRENT cue g:
//   with a cue (g !== null)   r = 1[next == g], done = (next == g)     -- the cue's declared meaning (CD-005)
//   without a cue             r, done = the last observed values for the pair
// and updates Q(node, g, action) exactly as QL does.
// ==========================================================
import { argmaxRandomTie, makeTable } from '../testbed/agent_util.mjs';

export const DYNAQ_DEFAULTS = Object.freeze({ alpha: 0.5, gamma: 0.95, epsilon: 0.1, k: 20 });

export function makeDynaQ({ rng, params = {} }) {
  const { alpha, gamma, epsilon, k } = { ...DYNAQ_DEFAULTS, ...params };
  const Q = makeTable();
  const model = new Map();
  const pairs = [];
  const update = (node, goal, width, action, reward, next, nextWidth, done) => {
    const r = Q.row(node, goal, width);
    const target = reward + (done ? 0 : gamma * Math.max(...Q.row(next, goal, nextWidth)));
    r[action] += alpha * (target - r[action]);
  };
  return {
    kind: 'DYNAQ',
    act(obs) {
      const r = Q.row(obs.node, obs.goal, obs.nActions);
      if (rng() < epsilon) return Math.floor(rng() * obs.nActions);
      return argmaxRandomTie(r, rng);
    },
    observe(tr) {
      const { obs, action, reward, next, done } = tr;
      update(obs.node, obs.goal, obs.nActions, action, reward, next.node, next.nActions, done);
      const key = `${obs.node}|${action}`;
      if (!model.has(key)) pairs.push([obs.node, action, obs.nActions]);
      model.set(key, { next: next.node, nextWidth: next.nActions, reward, done });
      const g = obs.goal;
      for (let i = 0; i < k; i++) {
        const [node, a, width] = pairs[Math.floor(rng() * pairs.length)];
        const m = model.get(`${node}|${a}`);
        const r = g !== null ? (m.next === g ? 1 : 0) : m.reward;
        const d = g !== null ? m.next === g : m.done;
        update(node, g, width, a, r, m.next, m.nextWidth, d);
      }
    },
    inspect: () => ({ q: Q.snapshot(), modelPairs: pairs.length }),
  };
}
````

### cognitive/agents/oracle.mjs

File: `cognitive/agents/oracle.mjs` · SHA-256 (content) `68a9b51b23bb5639da8426b6a644ce4f450ea7b005cd28ee8da43cc42828e6ee` · 787 bytes · 15 lines

````js
// ==========================================================
// ORACLE — privileged upper-bound reference (never a learner; results labelled ORACLE)
// ==========================================================
// Declares privileged: true, so the runner hands it oracle.bestAction(): the shortest-path exit toward the true
// rewarded node on the true current graph.  With no rewarded node it acts uniformly at random.
// ==========================================================
export function makeOracle({ rng, oracle }) {
  if (!oracle) throw new Error('ORACLE needs the privileged channel');
  return {
    kind: 'ORACLE',
    privileged: true,
    act(obs) { const a = oracle.bestAction(); return a === null ? Math.floor(rng() * obs.nActions) : a; },
    observe() {},
  };
}
````

### cognitive/agents/ql.mjs

File: `cognitive/agents/ql.mjs` · SHA-256 (content) `9d8825a912c406e78df29a2ba5d5585d9c4292f32e7e0431dc01cbeb0b51d2f0` · 1534 bytes · 34 lines

````js
// ==========================================================
// QL — goal-conditioned tabular one-step Q-learning (model-free baseline)
// ==========================================================
// State = (node label, cue label or null).  Q starts at 0.  Policy: with probability epsilon a uniform exit, otherwise
// the greedy exit with ties broken uniformly.  Update after every real step:
//   Q(s,g,a) += alpha * (r + gamma * [not done] * max_a' Q(s',g,a') - Q(s,g,a))
// A truncated step bootstraps (truncation is not termination).  Nothing else writes Q.
// ==========================================================
import { argmaxRandomTie, makeTable } from '../testbed/agent_util.mjs';

export const QL_DEFAULTS = Object.freeze({ alpha: 0.5, gamma: 0.95, epsilon: 0.1 });

export function qTarget(table, tr, gamma) {
  const { reward, next, done } = tr;
  return reward + (done ? 0 : gamma * Math.max(...table.row(next.node, next.goal, next.nActions)));
}

export function makeQL({ rng, params = {} }) {
  const { alpha, gamma, epsilon } = { ...QL_DEFAULTS, ...params };
  const Q = makeTable();
  return {
    kind: 'QL',
    act(obs) {
      const r = Q.row(obs.node, obs.goal, obs.nActions);
      if (rng() < epsilon) return Math.floor(rng() * obs.nActions);
      return argmaxRandomTie(r, rng);
    },
    observe(tr) {
      const r = Q.row(tr.obs.node, tr.obs.goal, tr.obs.nActions);
      r[tr.action] += alpha * (qTarget(Q, tr, gamma) - r[tr.action]);
    },
    inspect: () => ({ q: Q.snapshot() }),
  };
}
````

// ==========================================================
// RANDOM — uniform over the exits of the current node (floor reference)

    kind: 'RANDOM',
    act(obs) { return Math.floor(rng() * obs.nActions); },

### cognitive/baselines/dynaq_plus.mjs

File: `cognitive/baselines/dynaq_plus.mjs` · SHA-256 (content) `6f4f3743e5a82b29ca24035518846552f4b0503fbb3586a3573737bf0ab5a5e6` · 4636 bytes · 83 lines

````js
// ==========================================================
// DYNAQP — Dyna-Q+ with a count model (Sutton & Barto 2018, section 8.3), the textbook baseline for recovery after
// a change
// ==========================================================
// The existing DYNAQ baseline keeps only the LAST outcome of each (node, action) and never re-tests anything.
// Dyna-Q+ adds the two things a fair model-free-with-model baseline needs here:
//
//   1. A count model.  For every experienced (node, action): how often each outcome followed (staying counts as an
//      outcome).  Planning samples the outcome from those counts, so stochastic edges are represented.
//   2. A bonus for actions not tried recently.  A planning update on (s, a) uses the reward
//          r + kappa * sqrt(tau)        tau = real steps since (s, a) was last tried in the real world
//      so an action left alone for long becomes worth re-testing.  As in the book, actions never tried at a visited
//      state may also be drawn in planning: they are modelled as leading back to the same state with reward 0, with
//      tau counted from the state's first visit.
//
// Real updates use the real reward without a bonus.  Reward semantics in planning are DYNAQ's: with a cue g,
// r = 1[next == g] and termination on arrival at g (the cue's declared meaning, CD-005); without a cue, the last
// observed reward and termination for that outcome.  Action choice is epsilon-greedy on Q, as for QL and DYNAQ.
// inspect().compute reports real steps and planning updates, so that work can be compared across agents.
// ==========================================================
import { argmaxRandomTie, makeTable } from '../testbed/agent_util.mjs';

export const DYNAQP_DEFAULTS = Object.freeze({ alpha: 0.5, gamma: 0.95, epsilon: 0.1, k: 20, kappa: 0.003 });

export function makeDynaQPlus({ rng, params = {}, kind = 'DYNAQP' }) {
  const { alpha, gamma, epsilon, k, kappa } = { ...DYNAQP_DEFAULTS, ...params };
  const Q = makeTable();
  const widths = new Map();          // node -> number of actions
  const firstSeen = new Map();       // node -> real step at which it was first observed
  const nodes = [];                  // visited nodes in first-visit order
  const model = new Map();           // `${node}|${a}` -> { n, last, out: Map(next -> { c, reward, done }) }
  const compute = { realSteps: 0, planningUpdates: 0 };
  let t = 0;
  const see = (node, width) => { if (!widths.has(node)) { widths.set(node, width); firstSeen.set(node, t); nodes.push(node); } };
  const update = (node, goal, action, reward, next, done) => {
    const r = Q.row(node, goal, widths.get(node));
    const target = reward + (done ? 0 : gamma * Math.max(...Q.row(next, goal, widths.get(next))));
    r[action] += alpha * (target - r[action]);
  };
  function sampleOutcome(m) {
    let u = rng() * m.n;
    for (const [next, o] of m.out) { u -= o.c; if (u < 0) return [next, o]; }
    const last = [...m.out].at(-1);
    return last;
  }
  return {
    kind,
    act(obs) {
      see(obs.node, obs.nActions);
      const r = Q.row(obs.node, obs.goal, obs.nActions);
      if (rng() < epsilon) return Math.floor(rng() * obs.nActions);
      return argmaxRandomTie(r, rng);
    },
    observe(tr) {
      const { obs, action, reward, next, done } = tr;
      see(obs.node, obs.nActions); see(next.node, next.nActions);
      t++; compute.realSteps++;
      update(obs.node, obs.goal, action, reward, next.node, done);
      const key = `${obs.node}|${action}`;
      const m = model.get(key) ?? { n: 0, last: t, out: new Map() };
      m.n++; m.last = t;
      const o = m.out.get(next.node) ?? { c: 0, reward: 0, done: false };
      o.c++; o.reward = reward; o.done = done;
      m.out.set(next.node, o); model.set(key, m);
      const g = obs.goal;
      for (let i = 0; i < k; i++) {
        const node = nodes[Math.floor(rng() * nodes.length)];
        const a = Math.floor(rng() * widths.get(node));
        const pm = model.get(`${node}|${a}`);
        let nx = node, r = 0, d = false, tau = t - firstSeen.get(node);
        if (pm) {
          const [sampled, out] = sampleOutcome(pm);
          nx = sampled; tau = t - pm.last;
          r = g !== null ? (nx === g && nx !== node ? 1 : 0) : out.reward;
          d = g !== null ? nx === g && nx !== node : out.done;
        }
        update(node, g, a, r + kappa * Math.sqrt(tau), nx, d);
        compute.planningUpdates++;
      }
    },
    inspect: () => ({ compute: { ...compute, updatesPerStep: compute.realSteps ? compute.planningUpdates / compute.realSteps : 0 }, modelPairs: model.size }),
  };
}
````

### cognitive/core/mbvi.mjs

File: `cognitive/core/mbvi.mjs` · SHA-256 (content) `b18cd55194d473e255e7e3299898e0d74ae9d1a1227bf89048b594095b626482` · 4168 bytes · 68 lines

````js
// ==========================================================
// CF-03 core — MBVI: learned-model planning agent (world model + value iteration + optimism)
// ==========================================================
// act(obs):     register the state, plan for the current cue with budgeted value iteration, act greedily on the
//               planned Q (ties uniform; epsilon only in the no-optimism ablation).
// observe(tr):  log model quality BEFORE learning (predictive log-likelihood of the realised outcome, and a
//               calibration bin for the modal non-self outcome), then update the world model.
// Reward: with a cue, the declared semantics R = D = 1[s' = cue]; without one, the learned arrival model.
// Values are cleared when the cue changes (a new goal is a new planning problem).
// inspect().compute reports decisions, sweeps, backups and the share of decisions that hit the budget.
// ==========================================================
import { argmaxRandomTie } from '../testbed/agent_util.mjs';
import { makeWorldModel, WM_DEFAULTS } from './world_model.mjs';
import { qValues, valueIteration } from './planner.mjs';

export const MBVI_DEFAULTS = Object.freeze({ gamma: 0.95, budget: 100, theta: 1e-6, vOpt: 1, epsilon: 0, ...WM_DEFAULTS });

export function makeMBVI({ rng, params = {}, kind = 'MBVI' }) {
  const P = { ...MBVI_DEFAULTS, ...params };
  const model = makeWorldModel(P);
  const V = new Map();
  let cue;                                   // undefined until the first act, so the first cue always (re)plans
  const compute = { decisions: 0, sweeps: 0, backups: 0, budgetHits: 0 };
  const quality = { n: 0, ll: 0, llUniform: 0, bins: Array.from({ length: 10 }, () => ({ n: 0, pred: 0, hit: 0 })) };
  const planOpts = () => ({
    gamma: P.gamma, vOpt: P.vOpt,
    rewardOf: cue !== null ? (s2) => (s2 === cue ? 1 : 0) : (s2) => model.rewardProb(s2),
    doneOf: cue !== null ? (s2) => (s2 === cue ? 1 : 0) : (s2) => model.doneProb(s2),
  });
  return {
    kind,
    act(obs) {
      model.visit(obs.node, obs.nActions);
      if (obs.goal !== cue) { cue = obs.goal; V.clear(); }
      const opts = planOpts();
      const r = valueIteration(model, V, { budget: P.budget, theta: P.theta, ...opts });
      compute.decisions++; compute.sweeps += r.sweeps; compute.backups += r.backups; if (!r.converged) compute.budgetHits++;
      if (P.epsilon > 0 && rng() < P.epsilon) return Math.floor(rng() * obs.nActions);
      return argmaxRandomTie(qValues(model, V, obs.node, opts), rng);
    },
    observe(tr) {
      const { obs, action, reward, next, done } = tr;
      model.visit(next.node, next.nActions);
      if (model.countOf(obs.node, action) >= 1) {
        quality.n++;
        quality.ll += Math.log(model.probOf(obs.node, action, next.node));
        quality.llUniform += Math.log(1 / (obs.nActions + 1));
        const p = model.predict(obs.node, action);
        if (p.outcomes.length) {
          const [modal, pm] = p.outcomes.reduce((b, x) => (x[1] > b[1] ? x : b));
          const bin = quality.bins[Math.min(9, Math.floor(pm * 10))];
          bin.n++; bin.pred += pm; bin.hit += next.node === modal ? 1 : 0;
        }
      }
      model.update(obs.node, action, next.node, reward, done);
    },
    inspect() {
      const total = quality.bins.reduce((s, b) => s + b.n, 0);
      const ece = total ? quality.bins.reduce((s, b) => s + (b.n ? Math.abs(b.hit / b.n - b.pred / b.n) * b.n : 0), 0) / total : null;
      return {
        compute: { ...compute, backupsPerDecision: compute.decisions ? compute.backups / compute.decisions : 0, sweepsPerDecision: compute.decisions ? compute.sweeps / compute.decisions : 0, budgetHitShare: compute.decisions ? compute.budgetHits / compute.decisions : 0, budget: P.budget },
        quality: { n: quality.n, meanLL: quality.n ? quality.ll / quality.n : null, meanLLUniform: quality.n ? quality.llUniform / quality.n : null, ece, calibrated: total },
        model: { states: model.states().size, ...model.stats() },
      };
    },
    _model: model, _V: V,                     // test access only (tests never pass these to an environment)
  };
}
````

### cognitive/core/planner.mjs

File: `cognitive/core/planner.mjs` · SHA-256 (content) `eceb06302e97bf97632a697558f0fc1b3ca9ee3e7d347cef6bdace36c5516835` · 1953 bytes · 41 lines

````js
// ==========================================================
// CF-03 core — budgeted value iteration over the learned world model
// ==========================================================
// Q(s,a) = p(SELF) * gamma * V(s)
//        + sum over known outcomes s'  p(s') * [ R(s') + gamma * (1 - D(s')) * V(s') ]
//        + p(UNKNOWN) * vOpt                                 (optimism: an unseen outcome is worth vOpt)
// V(s) = max_a Q(s,a), over every visited state.  R and D come from the caller: the learned arrival model, or the
// declared cue semantics R = D = 1[s' = cue] (CD-005).  Gauss–Seidel sweeps, warm-started from the caller's V, until
// the largest change is below theta or `budget` sweeps are used.  A backup is one Q(s,a) evaluation.
// ==========================================================

export function qValue(model, V, s, a, { gamma, vOpt, rewardOf, doneOf }) {
  const p = model.predict(s, a);
  let q = p.self * gamma * (V.get(s) ?? vOpt) + p.unknown * vOpt;   // an unswept state starts optimistic
  for (const [s2, pr] of p.outcomes) q += pr * (rewardOf(s2) + gamma * (1 - doneOf(s2)) * (V.get(s2) ?? vOpt));
  return q;
}

export function qValues(model, V, s, opts) {
  const w = model.widthOf(s);
  return Array.from({ length: w }, (_, a) => qValue(model, V, s, a, opts));
}

export function valueIteration(model, V, { budget, theta, ...opts }) {
  let sweeps = 0, backups = 0, delta = Infinity;
  const states = [...model.states()];
  while (sweeps < budget) {
    delta = 0;
    for (const [s, w] of states) {
      let best = -Infinity;
      for (let a = 0; a < w; a++) { const q = qValue(model, V, s, a, opts); if (q > best) best = q; }
      backups += w;
      const old = V.get(s);
      delta = Math.max(delta, old === undefined ? Infinity : Math.abs(best - old));
      V.set(s, best);
    }
    sweeps++;
    if (delta < theta) break;
  }
  return { sweeps, backups, converged: delta < theta };
}
````

### cognitive/core/world_model.mjs

File: `cognitive/core/world_model.mjs` · SHA-256 (content) `02bc7b21f325f6516c6774438c139076b118f17ca42cbe74ec0da573318369bf` · 4703 bytes · 87 lines

````js
// ==========================================================
// CF-03 core — learned world model (the agent's only knowledge of dynamics and reward)
// ==========================================================
// Learns from experienced transitions only: observation labels, action indices, observed rewards and done flags.
// It never sees the graph, reliabilities, distances or the rewarded node.
//
// Transitions, per (state s, action a): a Dirichlet over the outcome categories
//     SELF (stayed at s) · each observed non-self outcome s' · UNKNOWN (an outcome never observed for this pair)
// with prior pseudo-count alpha0 on SELF and on UNKNOWN.  Posterior predictive, N = observations of (s, a):
//     p(SELF) = (n_self + alpha0) / (N + 2 alpha0),  p(s') = n(s') / (N + 2 alpha0),  p(UNKNOWN) = alpha0 / (N + 2 alpha0)
// which sums to 1 for every pair, including untried pairs (N = 0: SELF 1/2, UNKNOWN 1/2).
//
// Reward and termination are modelled as functions of the arrival state (declared structural prior, CD-011):
//     P(reward | arrive at s') = (r1 + beta0) / (n + beta0),  P(done | arrive at s') = (d1 + beta0) / (n + beta0)
// an optimistic prior (mean 1 before any arrival).  A failed move (SELF) yields no reward and no termination.
//
// Change handling (surprise resets):
//   transition  if N >= resetMinN and the observed outcome had predictive probability < resetTau, the pair's counts
//               are cleared before the observation is added (a confident deterministic belief was contradicted)
//   reward      if n >= rewardResetMinN at the arrival state and the observed reward had predictive probability
//               < rewardResetTau, reward beliefs are cleared: for every state (globalRewardReset, the default:
//               one change-point for the whole reward function) or for that state only
// ==========================================================

export const WM_DEFAULTS = Object.freeze({
  alpha0: 0.25, resets: true, resetMinN: 20, resetTau: 0.05,
  beta0: 0.1, rewardResetMinN: 5, rewardResetTau: 0.05, globalRewardReset: true,
});

export function makeWorldModel(params = {}) {
  const P = { ...WM_DEFAULTS, ...params };
  const width = new Map();              // state label -> number of actions (observed on arrival)
  const pairs = new Map();              // `${s}|${a}` -> { N, nSelf, out: Map(label -> count) }
  const arrive = new Map();             // label -> { n, r1, d1 }
  const stats = { transitionResets: 0, rewardResets: 0, globalRewardResets: 0, updates: 0 };
  const pair = (s, a) => pairs.get(`${s}|${a}`);

  function predict(s, a) {
    const q = pair(s, a);
    const N = q ? q.N : 0, nSelf = q ? q.nSelf : 0, denom = N + 2 * P.alpha0;
    const outcomes = q ? [...q.out].map(([lab, c]) => [lab, c / denom]) : [];
    return { self: (nSelf + P.alpha0) / denom, outcomes, unknown: P.alpha0 / denom, N };
  }

  /** Predictive probability of the category the outcome s2 falls into (SELF, a known outcome, or UNKNOWN). */
  function probOf(s, a, s2) {
    const p = predict(s, a);
    if (s2 === s) return p.self;
    const hit = p.outcomes.find(([lab]) => lab === s2);
    return hit ? hit[1] : p.unknown;
  }

  const rewardProb = (s2) => { const r = arrive.get(s2); return r ? (r.r1 + P.beta0) / (r.n + P.beta0) : 1; };
  const doneProb = (s2) => { const r = arrive.get(s2); return r ? (r.d1 + P.beta0) / (r.n + P.beta0) : 1; };

  function update(s, a, s2, reward, done) {
    stats.updates++;
    const key = `${s}|${a}`;
    let q = pairs.get(key);
    if (q && P.resets && q.N >= P.resetMinN && probOf(s, a, s2) < P.resetTau) { q = null; stats.transitionResets++; }
    if (!q) { q = { N: 0, nSelf: 0, out: new Map() }; pairs.set(key, q); }
    q.N++;
    if (s2 === s) { q.nSelf++; return; }
    q.out.set(s2, (q.out.get(s2) ?? 0) + 1);
    const r = reward > 0 ? 1 : 0, d = done ? 1 : 0;
    const prev = arrive.get(s2);
    if (prev && P.resets && prev.n >= P.rewardResetMinN) {
      const pr = r ? rewardProb(s2) : 1 - rewardProb(s2);
      if (pr < P.rewardResetTau) {
        if (P.globalRewardReset) { arrive.clear(); stats.globalRewardResets++; } else { arrive.delete(s2); stats.rewardResets++; }
      }
    }
    const cur = arrive.get(s2) ?? { n: 0, r1: 0, d1: 0 };
    cur.n++; cur.r1 += r; cur.d1 += d;
    arrive.set(s2, cur);
  }

  return {
    params: P,
    visit(s, w) { if (!width.has(s)) width.set(s, w); else if (width.get(s) !== w) throw new Error(`state ${s} changed its action count`); },
    states: () => width,
    widthOf: (s) => width.get(s),
    predict, probOf, rewardProb, doneProb, update,
    countOf: (s, a) => pair(s, a)?.N ?? 0,
    stats: () => ({ ...stats }),
  };
}
````

### cognitive/mind/arb/agent.mjs

File: `cognitive/mind/arb/agent.mjs` · SHA-256 (content) `72b0f69fe88990209392388b3b0933c4b5f7f6141bc0baf2fded13dda1858ef8` · 4471 bytes · 78 lines

````js
// ==========================================================
// CF-04 mind — ARB: reliability arbitration between a planner and a habit learner (the mechanism under test)
// ==========================================================
// Two complete controllers learn from every transition:
//   planner  MBVI3 (world model v3 + value iteration), unchanged
//   habit    tabular one-step Q-learning with the QL baseline's update rule and parameters
// One selector chooses which controller's action is executed, and logs it.
//
// Reliability signals, each measured against that controller's own expected error so that irreducible noise does
// not count against it (the CF-03b review's objection to comparing raw errors):
//   planner  state-prediction error  spe = 1 - p_model(observed outcome), minus its expectation under the model's
//            own predictive distribution, 1 - sum_i p_i^2.  Mean zero when the model is right, positive when the
//            world has stopped behaving as the model says.
//   habit    |TD error| (clipped to 1), minus its own slow running average.
// Each excess is smoothed with rate `rate`.  The habit controller acts while
//       smoothed planner excess - smoothed habit excess > theta
// and the planner acts otherwise.
//
// Modes (params.mode):
//   RELIABILITY  the rule above                                   (kind ARB)
//   RANDOM       the habit acts with probability mfShare per decision, independently of any signal
//                (kind RANDSWITCH: the control for "any switching helps")
// inspect().control reports decisions, habit decisions and share, switches, disagreements (the two controllers
// proposed different actions) and decisive habit decisions (the habit acted AND disagreed with the planner).
// ==========================================================
import { argmaxRandomTie, makeTable } from '../../testbed/agent_util.mjs';
import { makeMBVI3 } from '../v3/agent.mjs';

export const ARB_DEFAULTS = Object.freeze({ alpha: 0.5, gamma: 0.95, epsilon: 0.1, rate: 0.1, slowRate: 0.01, theta: 0.05, mode: 'RELIABILITY', mfShare: 0 });

export function makeARB({ rng, params = {}, kind = 'ARB' }) {
  const P = { ...ARB_DEFAULTS, ...params };
  if (!['RELIABILITY', 'RANDOM'].includes(P.mode)) throw new Error(`unknown arbitration mode ${P.mode}`);
  const planner = makeMBVI3({ rng, params: P.planner ?? {} });
  const model = planner._model;              // the planner's own model, read for its predictive probabilities
  const Q = makeTable();
  const control = { decisions: 0, habitDecisions: 0, switches: 0, disagreements: 0, decisiveHabit: 0 };
  let eMB = 0, eMF = 0, slowMF = 0, last = null;
  return {
    kind,
    act(obs) {
      const aMB = planner.act(obs);
      const row = Q.row(obs.node, obs.goal, obs.nActions);
      const aMF = rng() < P.epsilon ? Math.floor(rng() * obs.nActions) : argmaxRandomTie(row, rng);
      const useHabit = P.mode === 'RANDOM' ? rng() < P.mfShare : eMB - eMF > P.theta;
      control.decisions++;
      if (useHabit) control.habitDecisions++;
      if (aMB !== aMF) { control.disagreements++; if (useHabit) control.decisiveHabit++; }
      if (last !== null && last !== useHabit) control.switches++;
      last = useHabit;
      return useHabit ? aMF : aMB;
    },
    observe(tr) {
      const { obs, action, reward, next, done } = tr;
      // planner signal, from the model as it stood BEFORE this transition
      const p = model.predict(obs.node, action);
      const expected = 1 - (p.self ** 2 + p.unknown ** 2 + p.outcomes.reduce((s, [, x]) => s + x ** 2, 0));
      const excessMB = (1 - model.probOf(obs.node, action, next.node)) - expected;
      // habit signal and update
      const row = Q.row(obs.node, obs.goal, obs.nActions);
      const delta = reward + (done ? 0 : P.gamma * Math.max(...Q.row(next.node, next.goal, next.nActions))) - row[action];
      const abs = Math.min(1, Math.abs(delta));
      const excessMF = abs - slowMF;
      slowMF += P.slowRate * (abs - slowMF);
      eMB += P.rate * (excessMB - eMB);
      eMF += P.rate * (excessMF - eMF);
      row[action] += P.alpha * delta;
      planner.observe(tr);
    },
    inspect() {
      const inner = planner.inspect();
      return {
        control: { ...control, habitShare: control.decisions ? control.habitDecisions / control.decisions : 0, signals: { planner: eMB, habit: eMF } },
        compute: inner.compute, model: inner.model,
      };
    },
  };
}
````

### cognitive/mind/mbvi2.mjs

File: `cognitive/mind/mbvi2.mjs` · SHA-256 (content) `67814c4a84f629202a134c79dca547e89ecb9123f9eec32ecab7149e54b2d0c1` · 4120 bytes · 68 lines

````js
// ==========================================================
// CF-03b mind — MBVI2: the CF-03 planning agent on world model v2
// ==========================================================
// Identical to cognitive/core/mbvi.mjs in every respect except the model it learns (mind/world_model.mjs): the
// planner, the optimism value, the greedy choice, the cue semantics and the quality log are the same code paths, so
// a difference between MBVI and MBVI2 on the same world and seeds is a difference between the two models.
//
// act(obs):     register the state, plan for the current cue with budgeted value iteration, act greedily on the
//               planned Q (ties uniform).
// observe(tr):  log model quality BEFORE learning (predictive log-likelihood of the realised outcome, and a
//               calibration bin for the modal non-self outcome), then update the world model.
// ==========================================================
import { argmaxRandomTie } from '../testbed/agent_util.mjs';
import { qValues, valueIteration } from '../core/planner.mjs';
import { makeWorldModel2, WM2_DEFAULTS } from './world_model.mjs';

export const MBVI2_DEFAULTS = Object.freeze({ gamma: 0.95, budget: 100, theta: 1e-6, vOpt: 1, ...WM2_DEFAULTS });

export function makeMBVI2({ rng, params = {}, kind = 'MBVI2' }) {
  const P = { ...MBVI2_DEFAULTS, ...params };
  const model = makeWorldModel2(P);
  const V = new Map();
  let cue;                                   // undefined until the first act, so the first cue always (re)plans
  const compute = { decisions: 0, sweeps: 0, backups: 0, budgetHits: 0 };
  const quality = { n: 0, ll: 0, llUniform: 0, bins: Array.from({ length: 10 }, () => ({ n: 0, pred: 0, hit: 0 })) };
  const planOpts = () => ({
    gamma: P.gamma, vOpt: P.vOpt,
    rewardOf: cue !== null ? (s2) => (s2 === cue ? 1 : 0) : (s2) => model.rewardProb(s2),
    doneOf: cue !== null ? (s2) => (s2 === cue ? 1 : 0) : (s2) => model.doneProb(s2),
  });
  return {
    kind,
    act(obs) {
      model.visit(obs.node, obs.nActions);
      if (obs.goal !== cue) { cue = obs.goal; V.clear(); }
      const opts = planOpts();
      const r = valueIteration(model, V, { budget: P.budget, theta: P.theta, ...opts });
      compute.decisions++; compute.sweeps += r.sweeps; compute.backups += r.backups; if (!r.converged) compute.budgetHits++;
      return argmaxRandomTie(qValues(model, V, obs.node, opts), rng);
    },
    observe(tr) {
      const { obs, action, reward, next, done } = tr;
      model.visit(next.node, next.nActions);
      if (model.countOf(obs.node, action) >= 1) {
        quality.n++;
        quality.ll += Math.log(model.probOf(obs.node, action, next.node));
        quality.llUniform += Math.log(1 / (obs.nActions + 1));
        const p = model.predict(obs.node, action);
        if (p.outcomes.length) {
          const [modal, pm] = p.outcomes.reduce((b, x) => (x[1] > b[1] ? x : b));
          const bin = quality.bins[Math.min(9, Math.floor(pm * 10))];
          bin.n++; bin.pred += pm; bin.hit += next.node === modal ? 1 : 0;
        }
      }
      model.update(obs.node, action, next.node, reward, done);
    },
    inspect() {
      const total = quality.bins.reduce((s, b) => s + b.n, 0);
      const ece = total ? quality.bins.reduce((s, b) => s + (b.n ? Math.abs(b.hit / b.n - b.pred / b.n) * b.n : 0), 0) / total : null;
      return {
        compute: { ...compute, backupsPerDecision: compute.decisions ? compute.backups / compute.decisions : 0, sweepsPerDecision: compute.decisions ? compute.sweeps / compute.decisions : 0, budgetHitShare: compute.decisions ? compute.budgetHits / compute.decisions : 0, budget: P.budget },
        quality: { n: quality.n, meanLL: quality.n ? quality.ll / quality.n : null, meanLLUniform: quality.n ? quality.llUniform / quality.n : null, ece, calibrated: total, bins: quality.bins.map((b) => ({ ...b })) },
        model: { states: model.states().size, ...model.stats(), ...model.hyper() },
      };
    },
    _model: model, _V: V,                     // test access only (tests never pass these to an environment)
  };
}
````

      return argmaxRandomTie(qValues(model, V, obs.node, opts), rng);

          const bin = quality.bins[Math.min(9, Math.floor(pm * 10))];
