````js
// M13 tests — the held-out modular-ring family and the generalization driver (experiments/m13_generalization).
// No agent is run on a modular world here: every agent run uses development-family worlds with labels m13test|*,
// which the M13 run never uses. The modular bank is checked for structure only.
// None of these tests may start the official run: the driver runs this suite itself before it starts.
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { generateWorld, mfwWorld, isConnected } from '../testbed/worlds.mjs';
import { runE5 } from '../testbed/task_e5.mjs';
import { GraphEnv } from '../testbed/env.mjs';
import { ADOPTED_AGENT, makeAdoptedAgent } from '../testbed/adopted_agent.mjs';
import { MODULAR, BANK_LABEL, modularEdges, buildWorld, makeBank, checkBank } from '../../experiments/m13_generalization/worlds_modular.mjs';
import { structureRecord } from '../../experiments/m13_generalization/structure.mjs';
import { returnMeasures, verifyAgainstRow } from '../../experiments/m13_generalization/measures.mjs';
import { pinDifferences } from '../../experiments/m13_generalization/pins.mjs';
import { ROOT, N_LABELS, ARMS, CORE, assertFreshLabels, runOne, readingRule, MARGIN } from '../../experiments/m13_generalization/m13_lib.mjs';

const HERE = path.join(ROOT, 'experiments', 'm13_generalization');
const DEV = 'm13test|dev|0';
let cache = null;
function runs() {           // five E5 runs on one development-family world (about 2 s each)
  if (cache) return cache;
  const W = generateWorld(DEV);
  cache = {
    W,
    belief: runOne(W, DEV, 'OFTEN', 'BELIEF', false),
    beliefTraced: runOne(W, DEV, 'OFTEN', 'BELIEF', true),
    mbvi3: runOne(W, DEV, 'OFTEN', 'MBVI3', false),
    mbvi3Traced: runOne(W, DEV, 'OFTEN', 'MBVI3', true),
    plain: runE5({ world: W, worldLabel: DEV, family: 'OFTEN', kind: ADOPTED_AGENT.kind, makeAgent: (_k, env, label) => makeAdoptedAgent(env, label) }),
  };
  return cache;
}
const strip = (row) => { const { arm, m13, ...rest } = row; return rest; };

export const tests = [
  ['M13 GENERATOR: a modular ring is a pure function of its label, with 4 modules of 6 nodes, 8 edges inside each, one bridge to the next module in a ring, degree at most 4', () => {
    for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
      const l = BANK_LABEL(i), a = modularEdges(l), b = modularEdges(l);
      assert.deepEqual(a, b, 'deterministic');
      assert.equal(a.n, 24); assert.equal(a.edges.length, 36);
      const deg = new Array(24).fill(0), mod = (u) => Math.floor(u / MODULAR.moduleSize), inside = [0, 0, 0, 0], bridges = new Set();
      for (const [u, v] of a.edges) { deg[u]++; deg[v]++; if (mod(u) === mod(v)) inside[mod(u)]++; else bridges.add([mod(u), mod(v)].sort().join('-')); }
      assert.ok(deg.every((d) => d >= 1 && d <= MODULAR.maxDegree), 'degrees in [1, 4]');
      assert.deepEqual(inside, [8, 8, 8, 8], 'eight edges inside every module');
      assert.deepEqual([...bridges].sort(), ['0-1', '0-3', '1-2', '2-3'], 'one bridge between consecutive modules, in a ring');
      assert.ok(isConnected(buildWorld(l, a.n, a.edges)));
    }
  }],
  ['M13 BUILDER: buildWorld gives exactly the testbed world (generateWorld, mfwWorld) for the same edges', () => {
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
      const l = `cf|dev|world|${i}`, g = generateWorld(l);
      assert.deepStrictEqual(buildWorld(l, g.n, g.edges), g);
    }
    const app = mfwWorld(path.join(ROOT, 'connections.json'), 'm13test|app');
    assert.deepStrictEqual(buildWorld('m13test|app', app.n, app.edges), app);
  }],
  ['M13 BANK: graph_bank.json is exactly the generator for m13|modular|0..164; structure.json is exactly its record; the novelty check holds', () => {
    const bank = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(HERE, 'graph_bank.json'), 'utf8'));
    assert.equal(bank.count, N_LABELS);
    assert.ok(checkBank(bank));
    assert.equal(JSON.stringify(bank, null, 1) + '\n', fs.readFileSync(path.join(HERE, 'graph_bank.json'), 'utf8'));
    assert.ok(assertFreshLabels(bank.graphs.map((g) => g.label)));
    const record = structureRecord(makeBank(N_LABELS));
    assert.equal(JSON.stringify(record, null, 1) + '\n', fs.readFileSync(path.join(HERE, 'structure.json'), 'utf8'));
    assert.equal(record.novelty.holds, true);
    assert.ok(record.novelty.bankClusteringP05 > record.novelty.devClusteringP95);
  }],
  ['M13 NAMESPACE: only fresh m13|modular|* labels pass; every earlier namespace is refused', () => {
    for (const bad of ['cf|dev|world|1', 'cf|gate6|world|0', 'mfw|m11|0', 'mfw|m11test|0', 'm13survey|modular|0', 'm13smoke|dev|0', 'm13test|dev|0', 'm13|structure|app|0']) assert.throws(() => assertFreshLabels([bad]));
    assert.throws(() => assertFreshLabels([BANK_LABEL(0), BANK_LABEL(0)]), /duplicate/);
    assert.ok(assertFreshLabels([BANK_LABEL(0), BANK_LABEL(164)]));
  }],
  ['M13 ARMS: BELIEF is the adopted agent; the diagnostic arms carry exactly its parameters plus the archive filter; MBVI3 is the registry baseline', () => {
    assert.equal(ARMS.BELIEF.kind, ADOPTED_AGENT.kind); assert.equal(ARMS.MBVI3.kind, 'MBVI3');
    assert.deepEqual(CORE, ['BELIEF', 'MBVI3']);
    const W = generateWorld(DEV), ctx = { isBlockedPair: () => false };
    const p = (arm) => ARMS[arm].make(new GraphEnv(W, { seedLabel: `${DEV}|${arm}` }), `${DEV}|agent`, ctx)._model.params;
    const base = p('BELIEF');
    assert.equal(base.rhoFixed, 0.5); assert.equal(base.volHazard, 0.01); assert.equal(base.archiveIf, undefined);
    for (const arm of ['BELIEF_REALONLY', 'BELIEF_FALSEONLY']) {
      const q = p(arm);
      assert.equal(typeof q.archiveIf, 'function');
      const { archiveIf, ...rest } = q, { archiveIf: _none, ...baseRest } = base;
      assert.deepEqual(rest, baseRest, `${arm} has the adopted parameters`);
    }
  }],
  ['M13 MEASURES: met, own-window and later crossings and the return delay on a hand-made trace', () => {
    const W = buildWorld('m13test|tiny', 4, [[0, 1], [1, 2], [2, 3], [0, 3]]);
    const a = (s, v) => W.exitOrder[s].indexOf(v);
    const step = (s, v, ok) => [0, 0, s, a(s, v), ok ? v : s, 0];
    // two training steps, then event 0 (edge 0-1, reverses): blocked 3 steps, after 3 steps; event 1 (edge 2-3,
    // permanent): blocked 2 steps, after 2 steps
    const trace = [step(0, 3, true), step(3, 0, true),
      step(0, 1, false), step(0, 3, true), step(3, 2, true),          // event 0 blocked: met (a failed try on 0-1)
      step(2, 1, true), step(1, 2, true), step(2, 1, true),           // event 0 after: no crossing of 0-1
      step(1, 2, true), step(2, 3, false),                            // event 1 blocked
      step(2, 1, true), step(1, 0, true)];                            // event 1 after: crosses 0-1 (later)
    const plan = { events: [{ edge: [0, 1], reverses: true }, { edge: [2, 3], reverses: false }] };
    const row = { world: 'tiny', family: 'X', metric: 10, events: [{ blocked: 3, after: 3, crossed: 0, ownAfterAttempts: 0 }, { blocked: 2, after: 2, ownAfterAttempts: 0 }] };
    const m = returnMeasures(W, trace, row, plan);
    assert.equal(m.reversing, 1); assert.equal(m.met, 1); assert.equal(m.metAndReturned, 1);
    assert.equal(m.returnedOwn, 0); assert.equal(m.returnedOnlyLater, 1); assert.equal(m.crossingsLater, 1);
    assert.deepEqual(m.delays, [11 - 5]);
    assert.ok(verifyAgainstRow(m, row));
    assert.throws(() => verifyAgainstRow(m, { ...row, events: [{ ...row.events[0], crossed: 1 }, row.events[1]] }), /crossings/);
  }],
  ['M13 MEASURES on real runs agree with the task\'s own counts, for BELIEF and MBVI3', () => {
    const r = runs();
    for (const x of [r.belief, r.mbvi3]) {
      assert.ok(x.row.m13 && x.row.m13.events.length === x.row.events.filter((e) => !e.skipped).length);
      assert.ok(verifyAgainstRow(x.row.m13, x.row));
    }
  }],
  ['M13 PASSIVE: keeping the environment reference and the recorder change no row', () => {
    const r = runs();
    assert.equal(JSON.stringify(r.belief.row), JSON.stringify(r.beliefTraced.row), 'BELIEF traced = untraced');
    assert.equal(JSON.stringify(r.mbvi3.row), JSON.stringify(r.mbvi3Traced.row), 'MBVI3 traced = untraced');
    assert.equal(JSON.stringify(strip(r.belief.row)), JSON.stringify(r.plain), 'runOne = runE5 with the adopted agent');
  }],
  ['M13 READING RULE: five independent lines and the summary statement of SPEC.md, on synthetic inputs', () => {
    const c = (mean, lo, hi) => ({ n: 165, mean, lo, hi });
    const ok = { identical: true, testsPassed: true, novelty: true, noExclusions: true };
    const A = (g1, g2, g3, g4, fails = []) => ({ criteria: { G1_mix: g1, G2_RARE_cost: g2, G3_OFTEN_afterReversed: g3, G4_OFTEN_metAndReturned: g4 }, gainShape: { G1_mix: { fails }, G3_OFTEN_afterReversed: { fails: [] } } });
    const all = readingRule(A(c(30, 10, 50), c(5, 1, 9), c(10, 4, 16), c(0.5, 0.2, 0.8)), ok);
    assert.deepEqual(all.lines, { validity: 'VALID', netAdvantage: 'RETAINED', safety: 'SAFE', recovery: 'SHOWN', behaviouralMechanism: 'SHOWN' });
    assert.equal(all.summary, 'NET ADVANTAGE RETAINED ON THE MODULAR FAMILY, WITHIN THE SAFETY MARGIN');
    // a G3 or G4 failure does not erase a retained net advantage
    const noMech = readingRule(A(c(30, 10, 50), c(5, 1, 9), c(1, -2, 4), c(0.1, -0.1, 0.3)), ok);
    assert.equal(noMech.summary, 'NET ADVANTAGE RETAINED ON THE MODULAR FAMILY, WITHIN THE SAFETY MARGIN');
    assert.equal(noMech.lines.recovery, 'NOT SHOWN'); assert.equal(noMech.lines.behaviouralMechanism, 'NOT SHOWN');
    // and a G3 or G4 pass does not create one
    const mechOnly = readingRule(A(c(3, -2, 8), c(5, 1, 9), c(10, 4, 16), c(0.5, 0.2, 0.8)), ok);
    assert.equal(mechOnly.summary, 'NET ADVANTAGE NOT SHOWN ON THE MODULAR FAMILY'); assert.equal(mechOnly.lines.behaviouralMechanism, 'SHOWN');
    assert.equal(readingRule(A(c(30, 10, 50), c(20, 10, MARGIN), c(10, 4, 16), c(0.5, 0.2, 0.8)), ok).summary, 'NET ADVANTAGE RETAINED ON THE MODULAR FAMILY; SAFETY NOT SHOWN');
    assert.equal(readingRule(A(c(30, 10, 50), c(5, 1, 9), c(10, 4, 16), c(0.5, 0.2, 0.8), ['median 0']), ok).summary, 'NET ADVANTAGE RETAINED ON THE MODULAR FAMILY, WITHIN THE SAFETY MARGIN — FROM A MINORITY OF LABELS');
    assert.equal(readingRule(A(c(-9, -15, -3), c(5, 1, 9), c(10, 4, 16), c(0.5, 0.2, 0.8)), ok).summary, 'NET ADVANTAGE REVERSED ON THE MODULAR FAMILY');
    const bad = readingRule(A(c(30, 10, 50), c(5, 1, 9), c(10, 4, 16), c(0.5, 0.2, 0.8)), { ...ok, identical: false, noExclusions: false });
    assert.equal(bad.summary, 'INVALID — NO CRITERION IS INTERPRETED'); assert.equal(bad.lines.validity, 'INVALID (determinism, exclusions)');
    assert.equal(readingRule(A(c(0, 0, 0), c(5, 1, 9), c(10, 4, 16), c(0.5, 0.2, 0.8)), ok).lines.netAdvantage, 'NOT SHOWN', 'strict');
    assert.equal(readingRule(A({ n: 0, mean: null, lo: null, hi: null }, c(5, 1, 9), c(10, 4, 16), c(0.5, 0.2, 0.8)), ok).lines.netAdvantage, 'NOT SHOWN', 'empty never passes');
  }],
  ['M13 REFUSALS: the driver refuses an unknown domain and a smoke output inside the repository (no test can start the run)', () => {
    const drv = path.join(HERE, 'run_m13.mjs');
    const bad = spawnSync(process.execPath, [drv, '--domain', 'nope'], { cwd: ROOT, encoding: 'utf8' });
    assert.equal(bad.status, 2); assert.match(bad.stderr, /--domain must be smoke or m13/);
    const inside = spawnSync(process.execPath, [drv, '--domain', 'smoke', '--labels', '1', '--out', path.join(HERE, 'smoke.json')], { cwd: ROOT, encoding: 'utf8' });
    assert.equal(inside.status, 2); assert.match(inside.stderr, /outside the repository/);
    assert.ok(!fs.existsSync(path.join(HERE, 'smoke.json')));
  }],
  ['M13 PINS: PINS.json names exactly the files the driver imports, with their current hashes', () => {
    assert.deepEqual(pinDifferences(), []);
  }],
];
````

````js
// AD-13 tests: the adopted agent is exactly the CF-06 gate6 primary arm (BELIEF), built through the unchanged registry.
// Only a development world is run here; gate6 worlds are never run again.
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { ADOPTED_AGENT, makeAdoptedAgent } from '../testbed/adopted_agent.mjs';
import { AGENTS_V6 } from '../testbed/registry_v6.mjs';
import { AGENTS_V5 } from '../testbed/registry_v5.mjs';
import { armAgentV6 } from '../testbed/arms_v6.mjs';
import { PRIVILEGED } from '../testbed/runner.mjs';
import { GraphEnv } from '../testbed/env.mjs';
import { generateWorld } from '../testbed/worlds.mjs';
import { runE5 } from '../testbed/task_e5.mjs';

const HERE = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const GATE6_RESULT = path.resolve(HERE, '..', '..', 'experiments', 'cf06', 'results', 'gate6_result.json');
const LABEL = 'cf|dev|world|5', W = generateWorld(LABEL);

// Every effective parameter of the agent that ran on gate6 (code unchanged since the freeze 7ce1dc6).
const GATE6_EFFECTIVE = Object.freeze({
  poolMove: true, poolDest: true, hyperMean0: 0.5, hyperN0: 1, strength0: 2, strengthGrid: [1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64],
  emIterations: 25, piBlock: 0.05, kappa: 0.25, resets: true, changeHazard: 0.002, changeAlt: 0.9, changeMinN: 5,
  beta0: 0.1, rewardResetMinN: 5, rewardResetTau: 0.05, globalRewardReset: false,
  volHazard: 0.01, rhoA0: 1, rhoB0: 1, rhoFixed: 0.5, rhoIterations: 30,
  gamma: 0.95, budget: 100, theta: 1e-6, vOpt: 1,
});

export const tests = [
  ['ADOPTED AGENT (AD-13): the definition is the gate6 primary arm as recorded in the frozen result file', () => {
    const rec = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(GATE6_RESULT, 'utf8')).parameters;
    assert.equal(rec.primary, ADOPTED_AGENT.name);
    const [kind, params, , options] = rec.arms[rec.primary];
    assert.equal(kind, ADOPTED_AGENT.kind);
    assert.deepEqual(params, { ...ADOPTED_AGENT.params });
    assert.equal(options, undefined, 'an ordinary arm, not a diagnostic one');
    assert.ok(Object.isFrozen(ADOPTED_AGENT) && Object.isFrozen(ADOPTED_AGENT.params));
  }],
  ['ADOPTED AGENT: every effective parameter is the one that ran on gate6 (h = 0.01, rho 1/2); no oracle; the baseline is kept', () => {
    const agent = makeAdoptedAgent(new GraphEnv(W, { seedLabel: 'cf|test|ad13' }), 'cf|test|ad13|agent');
    assert.deepEqual({ ...agent._model.params }, { ...GATE6_EFFECTIVE });
    assert.equal(PRIVILEGED.includes(ADOPTED_AGENT.kind), false);
    assert.equal(agent.privileged, undefined);
    assert.equal(AGENTS_V6[ADOPTED_AGENT.baseline], AGENTS_V5.MBVI3, 'MBVI3 is the earlier factory, unchanged');
  }],
  ['ADOPTED AGENT: on an E5 development world it acts step for step as the gate6 arm path (armAgentV6) does', () => {
    const viaDefinition = runE5({ world: W, worldLabel: LABEL, family: 'OFTEN', kind: ADOPTED_AGENT.kind, makeAgent: (_k, env, label) => makeAdoptedAgent(env, label) });
    const viaGateArm = runE5({ world: W, worldLabel: LABEL, family: 'OFTEN', kind: 'VOL_RHO', makeAgent: armAgentV6(['VOL_RHO', { rhoFixed: 0.5 }]) });
    assert.ok(viaDefinition.secondary.volatility.opened > 0, 'the belief acted: changes were archived');
    assert.deepEqual(viaDefinition, viaGateArm);
  }],
];
````

## Appendix F. Sample size and power (no agent)

### experiments/m13_generalization/power/power_m13.mjs

File: `experiments/m13_generalization/power/power_m13.mjs` · SHA-256 (content) `5c1e55074d186516a9cc245c79ad2b59cff5cd9ecd3a6ed9a12bdfd031ce4141` · 11552 bytes · 149 lines

````js
// ==========================================================
// M13 — the sample-size and power calculation behind SPEC.md (design aid; reads stored rows and the frozen bank)
// ==========================================================
//   node experiments/m13_generalization/power/power_m13.mjs        writes power_m13.out and power_m13.json beside it
// No agent is built or run. It reads:
//   - the stored CF-06 gate6 rows and M11 rows (BELIEF and MBVI3), for the recorded spreads and shapes;
//   - the frozen graph bank, for the exclusions, which planE5 decides from the graph alone.
// Deterministic: every random draw comes from a labelled stream (testbed rng.mjs), and the bootstrap inside each
// simulated study is the study's own pairedBootstrap with 10,000 resamples.
//
// Statistical model (the G1 unit). For label i, d_i = sum over f in {RARE, HALF, OFTEN} of
// steps_MBVI3(i, f) - steps_BELIEF(i, f). The labels are independent draws (each label is its own graph, layout,
// schedule, reliabilities and agent streams); the three families of one label share the graph and layout, so their
// differences may be correlated. G1 passes when the 95% paired percentile bootstrap interval of mean(d) lies above 0.
// With standardized effect delta = mu / sigma, where sigma is the SD of d_i (the sum, so it already contains the
// covariances between the families), the normal approximation gives
//     power(n) = Phi(delta * sqrt(n) - z_0.975)        n_required = ceil(((z_0.975 + z_0.80) / delta)^2).
// The simulation checks that approximation for the percentile bootstrap, with normal and with the recorded skewed
// shapes of d.
// ==========================================================
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { makeStream } from '../../../cognitive/testbed/rng.mjs';
import { pairedBootstrap } from '../../../cognitive/testbed/stats.mjs';
import { planE5 } from '../../../cognitive/testbed/task_e5.mjs';
import { buildWorld } from '../worlds_modular.mjs';

const HERE = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const ROOT = path.resolve(HERE, '..', '..', '..');
const lines = [], out = {};
const say = (s = '') => { lines.push(s); console.log(s); };
const f4 = (x) => (x === null ? 'null' : x.toFixed(4));

// ---- normal distribution ----
const Z975 = 1.959963984540054, Z80 = 0.8416212335729143;
function Phi(x) {             // standard normal CDF via the Numerical Recipes erfcc Chebyshev fit (fractional error < 1.2e-7)
  const z = Math.abs(x) / Math.SQRT2, t = 1 / (1 + 0.5 * z);
  const r = t * Math.exp(-z * z - 1.26551223 + t * (1.00002368 + t * (0.37409196 + t * (0.09678418 + t * (-0.18628806 + t * (0.27886807 + t * (-1.13520398 + t * (1.48851587 + t * (-0.82215223 + t * 0.17087277)))))))));
  return x >= 0 ? 1 - r / 2 : r / 2;
}
const power = (delta, n) => Phi(delta * Math.sqrt(n) - Z975);
const nRequired = (delta, pw = 0.8) => { const z = pw === 0.8 ? Z80 : null; return Math.ceil(((Z975 + z) / delta) ** 2); };

// ---- 0. exclusions, from the frozen bank (planE5 reads only the graph) ----
const bank = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(HERE, '..', 'graph_bank.json'), 'utf8'));
const FAM = ['RARE', 'HALF', 'OFTEN'];
let excludedPairs = 0, excludedLabels = new Set(), skippedEvents = 0;
for (const g of bank.graphs) {
  const w = buildWorld(g.label, g.n, g.edges);
  for (const f of FAM) { const p = planE5(w, g.label, f); if (!p) { excludedPairs++; excludedLabels.add(g.label); } else skippedEvents += p.events.filter((e) => e.skipped).length; }
}
const nAnalysed = bank.count - excludedLabels.size;
out.exclusions = { bankLabels: bank.count, excludedPairs, excludedLabels: excludedLabels.size, analysedLabelsG1: nAnalysed, skippedEvents };
say('== 0. Exclusions, decided from the frozen bank by planE5 (graph only; no agent)');
say(`   ${bank.count} labels x 3 families: ${excludedPairs} (label, family) excluded, ${excludedLabels.size} labels lost to G1; analysed n = ${nAnalysed}; skipped events ${skippedEvents}`);

// ---- 1. analytic ----
const SESOI = 0.22;
say('\n== 1. Analytic (normal approximation to the paired percentile bootstrap)');
say(`   z_0.975 = ${Z975}, z_0.80 = ${Z80}`);
say(`   n_required(delta = ${SESOI}, 80%) = ceil(((${Z975.toFixed(6)} + ${Z80.toFixed(6)}) / ${SESOI})^2) = ceil(${(((Z975 + Z80) / SESOI) ** 2).toFixed(4)}) = ${nRequired(SESOI)}`);
const curve = {};
for (const d of [0.15, 0.2, 0.22, 0.25, 0.3, 0.444, 0.498]) curve[d] = Object.fromEntries([116, 150, 163, 165].map((n) => [n, +power(d, n).toFixed(4)]));
say('   power(delta, n):   n = 116 (the old worst case, 10% excluded) | 150 | 163 | 165');
for (const [d, r] of Object.entries(curve)) say(`      delta ${String(d).padEnd(5)}  ${Object.values(r).map((x) => x.toFixed(4)).join('  ')}`);
out.analytic = { z975: Z975, z80: Z80, sesoi: SESOI, nRequired: nRequired(SESOI), powerCurve: curve };

// ---- 2. recorded inputs ----
const g = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, 'experiments/cf06/results/gate6_result.json')));
const m = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, 'experiments/m11_routes/results/m11_result.json')));
const mean = (xs) => xs.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / xs.length;
const sd = (xs) => { const mu = mean(xs); return Math.sqrt(xs.reduce((s, x) => s + (x - mu) ** 2, 0) / (xs.length - 1)); };
const corr = (a, b) => { const ma = mean(a), mb = mean(b); let s = 0; for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) s += (a[i] - ma) * (b[i] - mb); return s / (a.length - 1) / (sd(a) * sd(b)); };
function vectors(rows, key) {
  const by = (fam, arm) => new Map(rows.filter((r) => r.family === fam && r[key] === arm && !r.excluded).map((r) => [r.world, r]));
  const C = FAM.map((f) => [by(f, 'BELIEF'), by(f, 'MBVI3')]);
  const labels = [...C[0][0].keys()].filter((w) => C.every(([B, M]) => B.has(w) && M.has(w))).sort();
  return labels.map((w) => {
    const fam = C.map(([B, M]) => M.get(w).metric - B.get(w).metric);
    const [B3, M3] = C[2], [B1, M1] = C[0];
    return { G1: fam[0] + fam[1] + fam[2], RARE: fam[0], HALF: fam[1], OFTEN: fam[2], G2: B1.get(w).metric - M1.get(w).metric, G3: M3.get(w).windows.afterReversed - B3.get(w).windows.afterReversed };
  });
}
const sources = { CF06_gate6: vectors(g.rows.e5, 'name'), M11_app_graph: vectors(m.rows, 'arm') };
say('\n== 2. Recorded inputs (stored rows; BELIEF and MBVI3)');
out.recorded = {};
for (const [name, V] of Object.entries(sources)) {
  const col = (k) => V.map((v) => v[k]);
  const sF = FAM.map((f) => sd(col(f))), r = { RH: corr(col('RARE'), col('HALF')), RO: corr(col('RARE'), col('OFTEN')), HO: corr(col('HALF'), col('OFTEN')) };
  const varSumFromParts = sF[0] ** 2 + sF[1] ** 2 + sF[2] ** 2 + 2 * (r.RH * sF[0] * sF[1] + r.RO * sF[0] * sF[2] + r.HO * sF[1] * sF[2]);
  const rec = {
    n: V.length, G1: { mean: mean(col('G1')), sd: sd(col('G1')), standardized: mean(col('G1')) / sd(col('G1')) },
    familySD: Object.fromEntries(FAM.map((f, i) => [f, sF[i]])), familyCorrelation: r,
    sdOfSumFromParts: Math.sqrt(varSumFromParts), sdIfIndependent: Math.sqrt(sF[0] ** 2 + sF[1] ** 2 + sF[2] ** 2),
    G2: { mean: mean(col('G2')), sd: sd(col('G2')) }, G3: { mean: mean(col('G3')), sd: sd(col('G3')), standardized: mean(col('G3')) / sd(col('G3')) },
  };
  out.recorded[name] = rec;
  say(`   ${name}: n = ${rec.n}; G1 mean ${rec.G1.mean.toFixed(2)}, SD ${rec.G1.sd.toFixed(2)}, standardized ${rec.G1.standardized.toFixed(4)}`);
  say(`      family SDs RARE ${sF[0].toFixed(2)}, HALF ${sF[1].toFixed(2)}, OFTEN ${sF[2].toFixed(2)}; correlations RARE-HALF ${r.RH.toFixed(3)}, RARE-OFTEN ${r.RO.toFixed(3)}, HALF-OFTEN ${r.HO.toFixed(3)}`);
  say(`      SD of the sum from the parts ${rec.sdOfSumFromParts.toFixed(2)} (= the G1 SD); it would be ${rec.sdIfIndependent.toFixed(2)} if the families were independent`);
  say(`      G2 (RARE cost) mean ${rec.G2.mean.toFixed(2)}, SD ${rec.G2.sd.toFixed(2)}; G3 (OFTEN after reopenings) mean ${rec.G3.mean.toFixed(2)}, SD ${rec.G3.sd.toFixed(2)}, standardized ${rec.G3.standardized.toFixed(4)}`);
}
const smaller = Math.min(out.recorded.CF06_gate6.G1.standardized, out.recorded.M11_app_graph.G1.standardized);
say(`   smallest effect of interest: half the smaller recorded G1 effect = ${(smaller / 2).toFixed(4)}, used as ${SESOI}`);

// ---- 3. simulation with the study's own bootstrap ----
const R = 2000;
function simulate(shapeName, n, delta) {
  const rnd = makeStream(`m13|power|${shapeName}|${n}|${delta}`);
  let draw;
  if (shapeName === 'normal') draw = () => { const u = Math.max(rnd(), 1e-12), v = rnd(); return delta + Math.sqrt(-2 * Math.log(u)) * Math.cos(2 * Math.PI * v); };
  else {   // the recorded G1 differences, standardized (mean 0, SD 1), shifted to the effect: the recorded shape, skew and tails kept
    const x = sources[shapeName].map((v) => v.G1), mu = mean(x), s = sd(x), z = x.map((v) => (v - mu) / s + delta);
    draw = () => z[Math.floor(rnd() * z.length)];
  }
  let pass = 0;
  for (let k = 0; k < R; k++) {
    const d = Array.from({ length: n }, draw);
    const b = pairedBootstrap(d, `m13|power|boot|${shapeName}|${n}|${delta}|${k}`);
    if (b.mean > 0 && b.lo > 0) pass++;
  }
  const p = pass / R;
  return { shape: shapeName, n, delta, reps: R, bootstrapResamples: 10000, power: p, monteCarloSE: Math.sqrt(p * (1 - p) / R) };
}
say(`\n== 3. Simulation: ${R} simulated studies per cell, each with pairedBootstrap (10,000 resamples), rule "mean > 0 and lower bound > 0"`);
out.simulation = [];
for (const shape of ['normal', 'CF06_gate6', 'M11_app_graph']) for (const n of [163, nAnalysed]) {
  const r = simulate(shape, n, SESOI);
  out.simulation.push(r);
  say(`   ${shape.padEnd(14)} n ${n}  delta ${SESOI}: power ${f4(r.power)} (Monte Carlo SE ${f4(r.monteCarloSE)}); analytic ${f4(power(SESOI, n))}`);
}

// ---- 4. the other criteria ----
say('\n== 4. The other criteria (analytic, normal approximation)');
const g2 = (cost, s, n) => Phi((25 - cost) / (s / Math.sqrt(n)) - Z975);
out.G2 = {};
for (const [name, rec] of Object.entries(out.recorded)) { out.G2[name] = { cost: rec.G2.mean, sd: rec.G2.sd, powerToPass: g2(rec.G2.mean, rec.G2.sd, nAnalysed) }; say(`   G2 (upper bound < 25), true cost and SD as in ${name} (${rec.G2.mean.toFixed(2)}, ${rec.G2.sd.toFixed(2)}): P(pass) at n ${nAnalysed} = ${f4(out.G2[name].powerToPass)}`); }
const sdMax = Math.max(...Object.values(out.recorded).map((r) => r.G2.sd));
const costLimit = 25 - (Z975 + Z80) * sdMax / Math.sqrt(nAnalysed);
out.G2.costPassedWith80pct = { sd: sdMax, cost: costLimit };
say(`   G2: with the larger recorded SD (${sdMax.toFixed(2)}), a true cost up to ${costLimit.toFixed(2)} steps passes with 80% probability`);
out.G3 = { powerAtSesoi: power(SESOI, nAnalysed), powerAtRecorded: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(out.recorded).map(([k, r]) => [k, power(r.G3.standardized, nAnalysed)])) };
say(`   G3 (secondary): power ${f4(out.G3.powerAtSesoi)} at delta ${SESOI}; at the recorded G3 effects: ${Object.entries(out.G3.powerAtRecorded).map(([k, v]) => `${k} ${f4(v)}`).join(', ')}`);
say('   G4 (secondary): no earlier record measured returns for MBVI3, so no spread is known and no power is claimed; the result reports its minimum detectable effect, 2.80 SD / sqrt(n)');
out.G4 = { note: 'no prior variance; power not claimed; MDE80 = (z_0.975 + z_0.80) SD / sqrt(n) reported with the result' };

fs.writeFileSync(path.join(HERE, 'power_m13.out'), lines.join('\n') + '\n');
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(HERE, 'power_m13.json'), JSON.stringify(out, null, 1) + '\n');
````

### experiments/m13_generalization/power/power_m13.out

File: `experiments/m13_generalization/power/power_m13.out` · SHA-256 (content) `41455764e5cbedd97ebd721e905b79b1ed55ec2f3ca8dc3e401805fbb8a19b1a` · 3169 bytes · 40 lines

````text
== 0. Exclusions, decided from the frozen bank by planE5 (graph only; no agent)
   165 labels x 3 families: 0 (label, family) excluded, 0 labels lost to G1; analysed n = 165; skipped events 1

== 1. Analytic (normal approximation to the paired percentile bootstrap)
   z_0.975 = 1.959963984540054, z_0.80 = 0.8416212335729143
   n_required(delta = 0.22, 80%) = ceil(((1.959964 + 0.841621) / 0.22)^2) = ceil(162.1669) = 163
   power(delta, n):   n = 116 (the old worst case, 10% excluded) | 150 | 163 | 165
      delta 0.15   0.3653  0.4511  0.4821  0.4868
      delta 0.2    0.5770  0.6878  0.7236  0.7288
      delta 0.22   0.6589  0.7687  0.8020  0.8068
      delta 0.25   0.7681  0.8647  0.8910  0.8946
      delta 0.3    0.8982  0.9568  0.9693  0.9709
      delta 0.444  0.9976  0.9997  0.9999  0.9999
      delta 0.498  0.9997  1.0000  1.0000  1.0000

== 2. Recorded inputs (stored rows; BELIEF and MBVI3)
   CF06_gate6: n = 135; G1 mean 84.68, SD 190.62, standardized 0.4442
      family SDs RARE 27.08, HALF 105.26, OFTEN 108.10; correlations RARE-HALF 0.161, RARE-OFTEN 0.203, HALF-OFTEN 0.472
      SD of the sum from the parts 190.62 (= the G1 SD); it would be 153.29 if the families were independent
      G2 (RARE cost) mean 17.40, SD 27.08; G3 (OFTEN after reopenings) mean 43.55, SD 69.55, standardized 0.6262
   M11_app_graph: n = 135; G1 mean 27.73, SD 55.65, standardized 0.4982
      family SDs RARE 12.80, HALF 32.84, OFTEN 44.84; correlations RARE-HALF 0.054, RARE-OFTEN -0.083, HALF-OFTEN -0.036
      SD of the sum from the parts 55.65 (= the G1 SD); it would be 57.03 if the families were independent
      G2 (RARE cost) mean 2.87, SD 12.80; G3 (OFTEN after reopenings) mean 11.07, SD 24.61, standardized 0.4497
   smallest effect of interest: half the smaller recorded G1 effect = 0.2221, used as 0.22

== 3. Simulation: 2000 simulated studies per cell, each with pairedBootstrap (10,000 resamples), rule "mean > 0 and lower bound > 0"
   normal         n 163  delta 0.22: power 0.7960 (Monte Carlo SE 0.0090); analytic 0.8020
   normal         n 165  delta 0.22: power 0.8060 (Monte Carlo SE 0.0088); analytic 0.8068
   CF06_gate6     n 163  delta 0.22: power 0.9125 (Monte Carlo SE 0.0063); analytic 0.8020
   CF06_gate6     n 165  delta 0.22: power 0.9110 (Monte Carlo SE 0.0064); analytic 0.8068
   M11_app_graph  n 163  delta 0.22: power 0.8420 (Monte Carlo SE 0.0082); analytic 0.8020
   M11_app_graph  n 165  delta 0.22: power 0.8455 (Monte Carlo SE 0.0081); analytic 0.8068

== 4. The other criteria (analytic, normal approximation)
   G2 (upper bound < 25), true cost and SD as in CF06_gate6 (17.40, 27.08): P(pass) at n 165 = 0.9500
   G2 (upper bound < 25), true cost and SD as in M11_app_graph (2.87, 12.80): P(pass) at n 165 = 1.0000
   G2: with the larger recorded SD (27.08), a true cost up to 19.09 steps passes with 80% probability
   G3 (secondary): power 0.8068 at delta 0.22; at the recorded G3 effects: CF06_gate6 1.0000, M11_app_graph 0.9999
   G4 (secondary): no earlier record measured returns for MBVI3, so no spread is known and no power is claimed; the result reports its minimum detectable effect, 2.80 SD / sqrt(n)
````

## Appendix G. Run provenance

### experiments/m13_generalization/results/run/run_m13_awake.ps1

File: `experiments/m13_generalization/results/run/run_m13_awake.ps1` · SHA-256 (content) `0362ae7f2edf5ff3ebe4b02c16a2cff51bdee128e60836df0349c70cb8d3ae40` · 1550 bytes · 19 lines

````powershell
# Runs the M13 generalization study once, holding a Windows "system required" execution state for the run's lifetime
# (the per-process request media players make; no setting is changed, and it is released when this script ends).
# Adapted from experiments/m11_routes/results/run/run_m11_awake.ps1.
param([string]$Repo, [string]$Log)
Add-Type -Namespace Win32 -Name Power -MemberDefinition '[DllImport("kernel32.dll")] public static extern uint SetThreadExecutionState(uint esFlags);'
$ES_CONTINUOUS = [uint32]2147483648
$ES_SYSTEM_REQUIRED = [uint32]1
$prev = [Win32.Power]::SetThreadExecutionState($ES_CONTINUOUS -bor $ES_SYSTEM_REQUIRED)
Set-Location $Repo
$head = (git rev-parse HEAD)
$dirty = @(git status --porcelain).Count
$now = { (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString('yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ssZ') }
"== $(& $now) m13 launch at HEAD $head, changed paths: $dirty, wake request held (previous state $prev), pid $PID" | Out-File -FilePath $Log -Encoding utf8
$proc = Start-Process -FilePath 'node' -ArgumentList 'experiments/m13_generalization/run_m13.mjs', '--domain', 'm13' -WorkingDirectory $Repo -RedirectStandardOutput "$Log.stdout" -RedirectStandardError "$Log.stderr" -NoNewWindow -PassThru
$null = $proc.Handle   # keep the handle so that ExitCode is available after the process ends
"== $(& $now) node pid $($proc.Id)" | Out-File -FilePath $Log -Append -Encoding utf8
$proc.WaitForExit()
"== $(& $now) exit $($proc.ExitCode)" | Out-File -FilePath $Log -Append -Encoding utf8
[Win32.Power]::SetThreadExecutionState($ES_CONTINUOUS) | Out-Null
````

### experiments/m13_generalization/results/run/hold_awake_until.ps1

File: `experiments/m13_generalization/results/run/hold_awake_until.ps1` · SHA-256 (content) `b232251f08672d39ccde653ec6f62ff7c89be20a47eb09cceb4f5df4e98f3bfe` · 540 bytes · 7 lines

````powershell
# Holds "system and display required" until the given process exits (no setting is changed; released at exit).
param([int]$WaitPid)
Add-Type -Namespace Win32 -Name Power2 -MemberDefinition '[DllImport("kernel32.dll")] public static extern uint SetThreadExecutionState(uint esFlags);'
$ES_CONTINUOUS = [uint32]2147483648
[Win32.Power2]::SetThreadExecutionState($ES_CONTINUOUS -bor [uint32]1 -bor [uint32]2) | Out-Null
Wait-Process -Id $WaitPid -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
[Win32.Power2]::SetThreadExecutionState($ES_CONTINUOUS) | Out-Null
````

## Appendix H. Independent check, written before the result

### experiments/m13_generalization/results/check/verify_m13.mjs

File: `experiments/m13_generalization/results/check/verify_m13.mjs` · SHA-256 (content) `89f077ad1c275e451258d33ebfecff23abf8825f7d8b3c64c2b36eba3a0459a5` · 17080 bytes · 179 lines

````js
// Independent check of experiments/m13_generalization/results/m13_result.json, written BEFORE the result was seen
// (2026-11-18, while the single run was in progress). It recomputes, with its own code, provenance, counts, every
// criterion and its bootstrap interval, the five verdict lines and the summary statement (SPEC.md "Verdict"),
// gainShape, the attribution (SPEC.md "Attribution"), G1's standardized effect and the detour-excess moderator.
// Shared with the driver: only pairedBootstrap (so that the interval seeds can be reproduced exactly) and planE5
// (the schedule; it reads only the graph).
// Illustration rule, fixed here before the result: label m13|modular|0, family OFTEN, the first reversing event whose
// edge BELIEF tried while it was blocked (in BELIEF's trace); for both core arms, the predicted chance the exit works
// at the first blocked try, just before the reopening and at the reopening, and the step of the first crossing after
// the reopening. If BELIEF met no reversing event there, the next traced label (1, then 2) is used.
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';

const ROOT = process.argv[2];
const imp = (p) => import(pathToFileURL(path.join(ROOT, p)).href);
const { pairedBootstrap } = await imp('cognitive/testbed/stats.mjs');
const { planE5 } = await imp('cognitive/testbed/task_e5.mjs');
const sha = (b) => crypto.createHash('sha256').update(b).digest('hex');
const git = (...a) => execFileSync('git', ['-C', ROOT, ...a]).toString().trim();
const fails = [];
const ok = (cond, msg) => { console.log(`${cond ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}  ${msg}`); if (!cond) fails.push(msg); };
const HERE = path.join(ROOT, 'experiments/m13_generalization');
const file = path.join(HERE, 'results/m13_result.json');
const raw = fs.readFileSync(file);
const j = JSON.parse(raw);
console.log(`result file sha256 ${sha(raw)}  (${raw.length} bytes)`);
const F = ['RARE', 'HALF', 'OFTEN'], ARMS = ['BELIEF', 'MBVI3', 'BELIEF_REALONLY', 'BELIEF_FALSEONLY'];
const sum = (xs) => xs.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0), mean = (xs) => sum(xs) / xs.length;
const sd = (xs) => { const m = mean(xs); return Math.sqrt(xs.reduce((s, x) => s + (x - m) ** 2, 0) / (xs.length - 1)); };
const median = (xs) => { const s = [...xs].sort((a, b) => a - b), n = s.length; return n % 2 ? s[(n - 1) / 2] : (s[n / 2 - 1] + s[n / 2]) / 2; };

// ---- provenance ----
const frozen = Object.fromEntries(fs.readFileSync(path.join(HERE, 'SPEC.sha256'), 'utf8').trim().split(/\r?\n/).map((l) => l.trim().split(/\s+/)).map(([h, n]) => [n, h]));
ok(j.specSha256 === sha(fs.readFileSync(path.join(HERE, 'SPEC.md'))) && j.specSha256 === frozen['SPEC.md'], `SPEC.md hash ${j.specSha256?.slice(0, 8)} = file = SPEC.sha256`);
ok(j.pinsSha256 === sha(fs.readFileSync(path.join(HERE, 'PINS.json'))) && j.pinsSha256 === frozen['PINS.json'], 'PINS.json hash = file = SPEC.sha256');
ok(j.worlds.bankSha256 === sha(fs.readFileSync(path.join(HERE, 'graph_bank.json'))) && j.worlds.bankSha256 === frozen['graph_bank.json'], 'graph bank hash = file = SPEC.sha256');
ok(JSON.stringify(j.structure) === JSON.stringify(JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(HERE, 'structure.json'), 'utf8'))) && sha(fs.readFileSync(path.join(HERE, 'structure.json'))) === frozen['structure.json'], 'structure record = structure.json = SPEC.sha256');
ok(j.headAtLaunch === j.headAtEnd && j.headAtLaunch.startsWith('8619d71'), `HEAD 8619d71 at launch and end (${j.headAtLaunch.slice(0, 7)}, ${j.headAtEnd.slice(0, 7)})`);
ok(j.appFiles['main.js'].startsWith('c92c5c6b6d2e32ae'), 'main.js is the audited file');
ok(j.tests && j.tests.exit === 0 && j.tests.passed === j.tests.total && j.tests.total >= 195, `test suite passed before the run (${j.tests?.passed}/${j.tests?.total})`);
ok(j.domain === 'm13' && j.labels === 165, 'domain m13, 165 labels');
ok(j.structure.novelty.holds === true && j.structure.novelty.bankClusteringP05 > j.structure.novelty.devClusteringP95, 'novelty check holds');
const A = j.parameters.arms;
ok(A.BELIEF.kind === 'VOL_RHO' && JSON.stringify(A.BELIEF.params) === '{"rhoFixed":0.5}' && JSON.stringify(A.BELIEF_REALONLY.params) === '{"rhoFixed":0.5}' && JSON.stringify(A.BELIEF_FALSEONLY.params) === '{"rhoFixed":0.5}' && A.MBVI3.kind === 'MBVI3' && JSON.stringify(A.MBVI3.params) === '{}', 'arms as specified (AD-13 parameters; MBVI3 baseline)');

// ---- counts and determinism ----
ok(j.rows.length === 165 * 3 * 4, `1,980 rows (${j.rows.length})`);
const key = (r) => `${r.world}|${r.family}|${r.arm}`;
ok(new Set(j.rows.map(key)).size === j.rows.length && ARMS.every((a) => j.rows.filter((r) => r.arm === a).length === 495), 'each (label, family, arm) exactly once');
ok(j.rows.every((r) => r.world.startsWith('m13|modular|')), 'every label in m13|modular|*');
const excluded = j.rows.filter((r) => r.excluded).length;
ok(excluded === 0 && j.validity.noExclusions === true && j.validity.excludedPairs === 0, `no exclusion (${excluded})`);
const core = j.rows.filter((r) => r.arm === 'BELIEF' || r.arm === 'MBVI3');
ok(j.determinism.identical && sha(JSON.stringify(core)) === j.determinism.rowsSha256RunA && j.determinism.rowsSha256RunA === j.determinism.rowsSha256RunB, 'core rows of run A hash to the recorded hash, identical to run B');
ok(j.traces.length === 18, `18 traces (${j.traces.length})`);
for (const t of j.traces) ok(t.steps.every(([, , , moved, blocked]) => !(moved && blocked)), `trace ${t.world.split('|')[2]} ${t.family} ${t.arm}: no blocked step moved (${t.steps.length} steps)`);
// measures: internal consistency and agreement with the task's own counts
let mOk = true;
for (const r of j.rows) {
  const m = r.m13, rev = m.events.filter((e) => e.reverses);
  if (!(m.metAndReturned <= m.met && m.met <= rev.length && m.returnedOwn + m.returnedOnlyLater <= rev.length)) mOk = false;
  for (const e of m.events) { const ev = r.events[e.k]; if (ev.ownAfterAttempts !== e.attemptsOwn || (e.reverses && ev.crossed !== e.crossedOwn)) mOk = false; }
  if (m.met !== rev.filter((e) => e.met).length || m.metAndReturned !== rev.filter((e) => e.met && (e.crossedOwn + e.crossedLater) > 0).length) mOk = false;
}
ok(mOk, 'behaviour measures consistent in every row and equal to the task\'s own counts');

// ---- criteria, recomputed ----
const cell = (f, arm) => new Map(j.rows.filter((r) => r.family === f && r.arm === arm).map((r) => [r.world, r]));
const labelsOf = (f) => [...cell(f, 'BELIEF').keys()].sort();
const pair = (f, X, Y, g) => { const a = cell(f, X), b = cell(f, Y); return labelsOf(f).map((w) => g(a.get(w)) - g(b.get(w))); };
const mixLabels = labelsOf('RARE');
const g1 = mixLabels.map((w) => F.reduce((s, f) => s + (cell(f, 'MBVI3').get(w).metric - cell(f, 'BELIEF').get(w).metric), 0));
const g2 = pair('RARE', 'BELIEF', 'MBVI3', (r) => r.metric);
const g3 = pair('OFTEN', 'MBVI3', 'BELIEF', (r) => r.windows.afterReversed);
const g4 = pair('OFTEN', 'BELIEF', 'MBVI3', (r) => r.m13.metAndReturned);
const crit = {
  G1_mix: pairedBootstrap(g1, 'm13|stats|m13|G1|mix'), G2_RARE_cost: pairedBootstrap(g2, 'm13|stats|m13|RARE|cost'),
  G3_OFTEN_afterReversed: pairedBootstrap(g3, 'm13|stats|m13|G3|OFTEN|afterReversed'), G4_OFTEN_metAndReturned: pairedBootstrap(g4, 'm13|stats|m13|OFTEN|metAndReturned'),
};
const same = (a, b) => a && b && a.n === b.n && Math.abs(a.mean - b.mean) < 1e-9 && a.lo === b.lo && a.hi === b.hi;
for (const [k, c] of Object.entries(crit)) ok(same(c, j.analysis.criteria[k]), `${k}: ${c.mean.toFixed(3)} [${c.lo.toFixed(3)}, ${c.hi.toFixed(3)}] n=${c.n} matches the driver`);

// ---- verdict lines and summary (SPEC.md "Verdict"), own implementation ----
const holds = (c) => c.n > 0 && c.mean > 0 && c.lo > 0;
const g0 = j.validity.identical && j.validity.testsPassed && j.validity.novelty && j.validity.noExclusions && mOk;
const shape = (d) => { const s = [...d].sort((a, b) => b - a), top = Math.ceil(0.1 * d.length), why = []; const inF = d.filter((x) => x > 0).length, ag = d.filter((x) => x < 0).length; if (!(inF > ag)) why.push('favour'); if (!(median(d) > 0)) why.push('median'); if (!(mean(s.slice(top)) > 0)) why.push('top'); return { inFavour: inF, against: ag, ties: d.length - inF - ag, median: median(d), topShare: sum(s.slice(0, top)) / sum(d), meanWithoutTop: mean(s.slice(top)), tail: why.length > 0, why }; };
const sh = { G1: shape(g1), G3: shape(g3) };
const lines = {
  validity: g0 ? 'VALID' : 'INVALID',
  netAdvantage: holds(crit.G1_mix) ? 'RETAINED' : crit.G1_mix.hi < 0 ? 'REVERSED' : 'NOT SHOWN',
  safety: crit.G2_RARE_cost.hi < 25 ? 'SAFE' : 'NOT SHOWN SAFE',
  recovery: holds(crit.G3_OFTEN_afterReversed) ? 'SHOWN' : 'NOT SHOWN',
  behaviouralMechanism: holds(crit.G4_OFTEN_metAndReturned) ? 'SHOWN' : 'NOT SHOWN',
};
const summary = !g0 ? 'INVALID — NO CRITERION IS INTERPRETED'
  : lines.netAdvantage === 'RETAINED' ? `NET ADVANTAGE RETAINED ON THE MODULAR FAMILY${lines.safety === 'SAFE' ? ', WITHIN THE SAFETY MARGIN' : '; SAFETY NOT SHOWN'}${sh.G1.tail ? ' — FROM A MINORITY OF LABELS' : ''}`
  : `NET ADVANTAGE ${lines.netAdvantage} ON THE MODULAR FAMILY`;
ok(JSON.stringify({ ...lines, validity: lines.validity.split(' ')[0] }) === JSON.stringify({ ...j.reading.lines, validity: j.reading.lines.validity.split(' ')[0] }), `lines recomputed: ${JSON.stringify(lines)}`);
ok(summary === j.reading.summary, `summary recomputed: ${summary}`);
ok((sh.G1.tail ? 'TAIL' : 'CONSISTENT') === (j.reading.gainShape.G1_mix.startsWith('TAIL') ? 'TAIL' : 'CONSISTENT') && (sh.G3.tail ? 'TAIL' : 'CONSISTENT') === (j.reading.gainShape.G3_OFTEN_afterReversed.startsWith('TAIL') ? 'TAIL' : 'CONSISTENT'), `gainShape recomputed: G1 ${sh.G1.tail ? 'TAIL-DRIVEN' : 'CONSISTENT'}, G3 ${sh.G3.tail ? 'TAIL-DRIVEN' : 'CONSISTENT'}`);

// ---- attribution (SPEC.md "Attribution"), own implementation ----
const mixOf = (arm) => mixLabels.map((w) => F.reduce((s, f) => s + (cell(f, 'MBVI3').get(w).metric - cell(f, arm).get(w).metric), 0));
const At = {
  A1: pairedBootstrap(pair('OFTEN', 'MBVI3', 'BELIEF_REALONLY', (r) => r.metric), 'm13|stats|m13|attr|OFTEN|real'),
  A2: pairedBootstrap(pair('OFTEN', 'MBVI3', 'BELIEF_REALONLY', (r) => r.windows.afterReversed), 'm13|stats|m13|attr|OFTEN|realAfter'),
  A3: pairedBootstrap(pair('OFTEN', 'MBVI3', 'BELIEF_FALSEONLY', (r) => r.metric), 'm13|stats|m13|attr|OFTEN|false'),
  A4: pairedBootstrap(mixOf('BELIEF_REALONLY'), 'm13|stats|m13|attr|mix|real'),
  A5: pairedBootstrap(mixOf('BELIEF_FALSEONLY'), 'm13|stats|m13|attr|mix|false'),
};
const D = j.analysis.secondary.attribution;
ok(same(At.A1, D.OFTEN.genuineChanges) && same(At.A2, D.OFTEN.genuineAfterReversed) && same(At.A3, D.OFTEN.falseAlarms) && same(At.A4, D.mix.genuineChanges) && same(At.A5, D.mix.falseAlarms), 'attribution contrasts A1-A5 match the driver');
const exact = j.rows.filter((r) => r.arm === 'BELIEF_REALONLY').every((r) => r.diagnostics.alarms.opensFalse === 0) && j.rows.filter((r) => r.arm === 'BELIEF_FALSEONLY').every((r) => r.diagnostics.alarms.opensReal === 0);
const lab = (gen, fal) => (!exact ? 'INVALID (the diagnostic split is not exact)' : gen && fal ? 'GENUINE REVERSALS AND FALSE-ALARM REPAIR' : gen ? 'GENUINE REVERSALS' : fal ? 'FALSE-ALARM REPAIR' : 'NOT ATTRIBUTED');
const src = lab(holds(At.A1) && holds(At.A2), holds(At.A3)), srcMix = lab(holds(At.A4), holds(At.A5));
ok(exact === D.splitExact && src === D.gainSource && srcMix === D.gainSourceMix, `attribution recomputed: split exact ${exact}; OFTEN ${src}; mix ${srcMix}`);

// ---- standardized effect and moderator ----
ok(Math.abs(mean(g1) / sd(g1) - j.analysis.secondary.standardizedEffectG1.value) < 1e-9, `G1 standardized effect ${(mean(g1) / sd(g1)).toFixed(4)} matches`);
const bank = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(HERE, 'graph_bank.json'), 'utf8'));
const bfs = (n, adj, t, cut) => { const d = new Array(n).fill(-1); d[t] = 0; let fr = [t]; while (fr.length) { const nx = []; for (const u of fr) for (const v of adj[u]) { const k = u < v ? `${u}-${v}` : `${v}-${u}`; if (d[v] < 0 && !cut.has(k)) { d[v] = d[u] + 1; nx.push(v); } } fr = nx; } return d; };
const { buildWorld } = await imp('experiments/m13_generalization/worlds_modular.mjs');
const mod = {};
for (const f of F) {
  const G = { 'excess 0': [], 'excess 1-3': [], 'excess 4+': [] }, B = cell(f, 'BELIEF'), M = cell(f, 'MBVI3');
  for (const g of bank.graphs) {
    const adj = Array.from({ length: g.n }, () => []); for (const [u, v] of g.edges) { adj[u].push(v); adj[v].push(u); }
    const p = planE5(buildWorld(g.label, g.n, g.edges), g.label, f), perm = new Set();
    p.events.forEach((e, k) => {
      if (e.skipped) return;
      const kk = e.edge[0] < e.edge[1] ? `${e.edge[0]}-${e.edge[1]}` : `${e.edge[1]}-${e.edge[0]}`;
      const x = bfs(g.n, adj, p.goal, new Set([...perm, kk]))[p.start] - bfs(g.n, adj, p.goal, perm)[p.start];
      if (!e.reverses) perm.add(kk);
      else G[x === 0 ? 'excess 0' : x >= 4 ? 'excess 4+' : 'excess 1-3'].push(M.get(g.label).events[k].after - B.get(g.label).events[k].after);
    });
  }
  mod[f] = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(G).map(([k, d]) => [k, { events: d.length, meanAfterDifference: d.length ? mean(d) : null }]));
}
const dm = j.analysis.secondary.moderatorDetourExcess;
ok(F.every((f) => Object.keys(mod[f]).every((k) => mod[f][k].events === dm[f][k].events && (mod[f][k].meanAfterDifference === null ? dm[f][k].meanAfterDifference === null : Math.abs(mod[f][k].meanAfterDifference - dm[f][k].meanAfterDifference) < 1e-9))), 'moderator recomputed with own BFS');

// ---- illustration (rule fixed above) ----
console.log('\nillustration:');
for (const L of ['m13|modular|0', 'm13|modular|1', 'm13|modular|2']) {
  const tb = j.traces.find((t) => t.world === L && t.family === 'OFTEN' && t.arm === 'BELIEF'), tm = j.traces.find((t) => t.world === L && t.family === 'OFTEN' && t.arm === 'MBVI3');
  const rb = cell('OFTEN', 'BELIEF').get(L), rm = cell('OFTEN', 'MBVI3').get(L);
  const ev = rb.m13.events.find((e) => e.reverses && e.met);
  if (!ev) { console.log(`  ${L}: BELIEF met no reversing event; next label`); continue; }
  const edge = tb.schedule[ev.k].edge, sameEdge = (f) => (f.from === edge[0] && f.to === edge[1]) || (f.from === edge[1] && f.to === edge[0]);
  console.log(`  ${L} OFTEN, event ${ev.k + 1}, edge ${edge.join('-')}: BELIEF delay ${ev.delay}; MBVI3 met ${rm.m13.events.find((e) => e.k === ev.k).met}, delay ${rm.m13.events.find((e) => e.k === ev.k).delay}`);
  for (const t of [tb, tm]) for (const f of t.followed.filter(sameEdge)) {
    const pts = f.points, first = pts.find((p) => p[2] === 1 && p[3] === 1), ri = pts.findIndex((p, i) => i > 0 && pts[i - 1][2] === 1 && p[2] === 0);
    console.log(`    ${t.arm} ${f.from}->${f.to}: first blocked try t=${first?.[0]} p=${first?.[1]}; just before reopening p=${ri > 0 ? pts[ri - 1][1] : '-'}; at reopening t=${ri >= 0 ? pts[ri][0] : '-'} p=${ri >= 0 ? pts[ri][1] : '-'}; tries after reopening ${ri >= 0 ? pts.filter((p) => p[0] >= pts[ri][0] && p[3] === 1).length : '-'}`);
  }
  break;
}

// ---- summaries for the record ----
console.log('\ncriteria (mean [lo, hi], n; sd; median; +/-/0):');
for (const [k, d] of [['G1_mix', g1], ['G2_RARE_cost', g2], ['G3_OFTEN_afterReversed', g3], ['G4_OFTEN_metAndReturned', g4]]) { const c = crit[k]; console.log(`  ${k.padEnd(24)} ${c.mean.toFixed(3)} [${c.lo.toFixed(3)}, ${c.hi.toFixed(3)}], n ${c.n}; sd ${sd(d).toFixed(3)}; median ${median(d)}; ${d.filter((x) => x > 0).length}/${d.filter((x) => x < 0).length}/${d.filter((x) => x === 0).length}`); }
console.log(`  G4 minimum detectable effect (80%): ${(2.801585 * sd(g4) / Math.sqrt(g4.length)).toFixed(3)}`);
console.log(`  gainShape G1 ${JSON.stringify(sh.G1)}; G3 ${JSON.stringify(sh.G3)}`);
console.log(`  standardized G1 ${(mean(g1) / sd(g1)).toFixed(4)} (CF-06 0.444, M11 0.498)`);
console.log('attribution:'); for (const [k, c] of Object.entries(At)) console.log(`  ${k} ${c.mean.toFixed(3)} [${c.lo.toFixed(3)}, ${c.hi.toFixed(3)}]`);
console.log(`  OFTEN ${src}; mix ${srcMix}; split exact ${exact}`);
console.log('moderator:', JSON.stringify(mod));
console.log('\nper family:');
for (const f of F) {
  const fa = j.analysis.families[f];
  console.log(`  ${f}: arms ${JSON.stringify(Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(fa.arms).map(([a, v]) => [a, Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(v).map(([k, x]) => [k, typeof x === 'number' ? +x.toFixed(3) : x]))])))}`);
  console.log(`     contrasts ${JSON.stringify(Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(fa.contrasts).map(([k, c]) => [k, [+c.mean.toFixed(3), +c.lo.toFixed(3), +c.hi.toFixed(3), c.positive, c.negative, c.zero]])))}`);
  console.log(`     belief archives ${JSON.stringify(Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(fa.beliefArchivesPerRun).map(([k, x]) => [k, +x.toFixed(3)])))}`);
}
console.log(`\nresources: ${JSON.stringify(j.resources)}`);
console.log(`\n${fails.length ? `FAILURES: ${fails.length}` : 'all checks pass'}`);
console.log('git status of the repository:', git('status', '--porcelain') || '(clean)');
````

## Appendix I. The results record

### experiments/m13_generalization/RESULTS.md

File: `experiments/m13_generalization/RESULTS.md` · SHA-256 (content) `309a51e347745095f3bfe6cf208ea7362edb70810aa30d4d37a1339d357dfa7c` · 22247 bytes · 379 lines

````markdown
# M13 — results: the adopted belief on a held-out modular-ring family

**Summary statement (by the frozen rule): NET ADVANTAGE RETAINED ON THE MODULAR FAMILY, WITHIN THE SAFETY MARGIN.**

| Line | Value |
|---|---|
| Validity (G0) | **VALID** |
| Net advantage (G1, primary) | **RETAINED**, +125.4 steps a label [92.0, 160.1] |
| Safety (G2) | **SAFE**, RARE cost +9.8 [3.6, 14.9], upper bound below 25 |
| Recovery (G3, secondary) | **SHOWN**, +65.1 [50.9, 79.9] |
| Behavioural mechanism (G4, secondary) | **SHOWN**, +1.46 [1.25, 1.67] reopened edges met and re-crossed |

gainShape is **CONSISTENT** for both G1 and G3.

Under the frozen wording rules (SPEC.md, "Verdict", rules 2 and 3), the record may say three things:
- **The advantage held:** the belief kept its advantage over MBVI3 on one held-out structural family, in one
  development-level run.
- **Recovery:** it recovers reopened routes faster on this family. Recovery is SHOWN, and the OFTEN attribution is
  GENUINE REVERSALS.
- **Behaviour:** its belief changes what it does after meeting a block. The behavioural mechanism is SHOWN.

The record may not say that the belief generalizes in general.

**Evidence category:** a development-level result. It is one run, by the designer, on local commits that have not been
pushed. It is not confirmatory and decides nothing about AD-13 or the agent ([SPEC.md](SPEC.md), "Consequence").

## 1. The run

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Specification | [SPEC.md](SPEC.md) revision 2, frozen in `8619d71` on the owner's word "Freeze and run"; SHA-256 `d4140479e470f2d5…b6c430`. Pins `0ffbbd84…` (44 files), bank `25e02ebb…`, structure record `92bb4a69…`, all equal to [SPEC.sha256](SPEC.sha256) |
| Launch and end | 2026-11-18 06:07:14Z to 08:16:28Z, at HEAD `8619d71` both times, with 0 changed paths at launch. The driver's own clock reads 7,530 s; the rest is the pre-run checks and the test suite |
| Command | `node experiments/m13_generalization/run_m13.mjs --domain m13`, detached, with a per-process wake request ([results/run/](results/run/)) |

| Result | [results/m13_result.json](results/m13_result.json), 11,841,004 bytes, SHA-256 `a0cd87d432d2dd93d4bc20d2f8274858c4ae3fdb3906fbac10f407f6e3390367` |
| App files | `main.js` `c92c5c6b…` (the audited file, untouched), `index.html` `be3ba6ce…`, `connections.json` `550bddd8…`, `neurons.json` `b9847140…` |
| Scale | Run A: 165 labels × 3 families × 4 arms = 1,980 E5 runs, with the recorder on labels 0–2. Run B: the 990 core runs again, untraced |
| Exclusions | 0 of 495 (label, family) pairs, as computed from the bank before the freeze |
| Skipped events | 1 of 2,970 (one RARE label, the same in every arm), as the bank predicted |
| Capped episodes | 0 in BELIEF and MBVI3. One each in BELIEF_REALONLY's RARE and OFTEN runs (a diagnostic arm; no criterion uses it) |
| Determinism | Run A and run B core rows are identical: one SHA-256, `86f0bd3f…a608e02d`, for both |
| Standby | None during the run. The System log ([results/run/m13_power_events.txt](results/run/m13_power_events.txt)) has one standby marker, a Kernel-Power 507 at 11:34:04 local, which is 3 minutes before the launch: the machine leaving standby on keyboard input. Inside the run window there are none, only power-source (charger) changes. The driver's progress lines are never more than 62 s apart |

**Independent check:** [results/check/verify_m13.mjs](results/check/verify_m13.mjs) was written before the result
existed. It uses its own code to recompute:
- provenance;
- the counts and uniqueness of the rows;
- determinism;
- "no blocked step moved", on all 18 traces;
- the behaviour measures against the task's own counts, in every row;
- G1–G4 with their bootstrap intervals;
- the five lines, the summary statement and gainShape;
- attribution A1–A5 with the exactness check;
- the standardized effect;
- the moderator, with its own shortest-path code.

Only `pairedBootstrap`, `planE5` and the world builder are shared, so that the interval seeds and the schedules match.
**All checks pass** ([results/check/verify_m13.out](results/check/verify_m13.out)). The check fixed its illustration
rule in advance (§8). After the result, `node scripts/test.mjs` is still 195/195.

## 2. The criteria

Each row is the per-label paired difference: mean, 95% paired percentile bootstrap interval (10,000 resamples, seeded
by `m13|stats|m13|…`), and n = 165.

| | Criterion | Statistic | Result | SD | Median | + / − / 0 | Passes when | |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **G0** | Validity | determinism, tests, novelty, no exclusion | all four hold | | | | all four | **VALID** |
| **G1** | Net advantage (primary) | Σ families, steps(MBVI3) − steps(BELIEF) | **+125.38 [92.00, 160.09]** | 222.9 | 44 | 102 / 62 / 1 | mean > 0, lower > 0 | **RETAINED** |
| **G2** | Safety | RARE, steps(BELIEF) − steps(MBVI3) | **+9.80 [3.56, 14.90]** | 37.2 | 14 | 133 / 29 / 3 | upper < 25 | **SAFE** |
| **G3** | Recovery (secondary) | OFTEN, after-reopening steps, MBVI3 − BELIEF | **+65.08 [50.92, 79.93]** | 95.8 | 23 | 124 / 22 / 19 | mean > 0, lower > 0 | **SHOWN** |
| **G4** | Behaviour (secondary) | OFTEN, reopened edges met and re-crossed, BELIEF − MBVI3 | **+1.461 [1.255, 1.673]** | 1.364 | 1 | 128 / 4 / 33 | mean > 0, lower > 0 | **SHOWN** |

**gainShape (CF-06's rule):**

| | Labels for / against | Median | Top decile's share of the sum | Mean without the top 17 labels | Reading |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| G1 | 102 / 62 | +44 | 51.7% | +67.6 | **CONSISTENT** |
| G3 | 124 / 22 | +23 | 44.7% | +40.1 | **CONSISTENT** |

The advantage is not carried by a few labels. Still, the most favourable tenth of the labels supplies about half of
G1's sum.

**G2 in context:** BELIEF does pay in RARE. It uses more steps in 133 of 165 labels, and its median cost is 14 steps.
The mean cost (9.8) is 0.66% of MBVI3's RARE steps, and the whole interval is below the 25-step margin.

**G4's minimum detectable effect** at 80% power (SPEC: 2.80 · SD/√n) is 2.80 × 1.364 / √165 = **0.297**. The
observed +1.461 is about five times that.

## 3. All contrasts

Steps are summed over the change phase of one run. A positive value favours BELIEF in every row except three: `cost`
and `futileRetests` are BELIEF's extra steps or re-tests, and the return rows count BELIEF − MBVI3 events. The
intervals here use their own seeds (`m13|stats|m13|<family>|<name>`). G3's row therefore differs from §2 in the second
decimal.

| Family | Contrast | Mean [95% interval] | Median | + / − / 0 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| RARE | total (MBVI3 − BELIEF) | −9.80 [−14.96, −3.64] | −14 | 29 / 133 / 3 |
| RARE | cost (BELIEF − MBVI3) | +9.80 [3.56, 14.90] | 14 | 133 / 29 / 3 |
| RARE | blocked | −11.03 [−14.20, −7.55] | −10 | 22 / 141 / 2 |
| RARE | afterReversed | +1.78 [0.71, 3.02] | 0 | 22 / 11 / 132 |
| RARE | afterPermanent | −0.55 [−3.12, 2.35] | −3 | 39 / 119 / 7 |
| RARE | futileRetests (BELIEF − MBVI3) | +3.10 [1.72, 4.34] | 4 | 135 / 25 / 5 |
| RARE | metAndReturned (BELIEF − MBVI3) | +0.09 [0.04, 0.15] | 0 | 14 / 1 / 150 |
| HALF | total | +52.61 [32.58, 74.38] | −1 | 76 / 84 / 5 |
| HALF | cost | −52.61 [−74.22, −32.90] | 1 | 84 / 76 / 5 |
| HALF | blocked | +9.33 [1.13, 18.64] | −5 | 53 / 105 / 7 |
| HALF | afterReversed | +32.76 [23.85, 42.80] | 6 | 107 / 25 / 33 |
| HALF | afterPermanent | +10.51 [5.41, 16.07] | −1 | 58 / 88 / 19 |
| HALF | futileRetests | +2.82 [1.66, 4.05] | 2 | 126 / 23 / 16 |
| HALF | metAndReturned | +0.84 [0.69, 0.98] | 1 | 89 / 0 / 76 |
| OFTEN | total | +82.57 [60.35, 105.90] | 17 | 96 / 57 / 12 |
| OFTEN | cost | −82.57 [−106.22, −60.67] | −17 | 57 / 96 / 12 |
| OFTEN | blocked | +15.45 [5.54, 26.24] | 0 | 72 / 79 / 14 |
| OFTEN | afterReversed | +65.08 [50.92, 80.13] | 23 | 124 / 22 / 19 |
| OFTEN | afterPermanent | +2.04 [0.28, 4.10] | 0 | 25 / 30 / 110 |
| OFTEN | futileRetests | +1.12 [0.13, 2.21] | 0 | 45 / 6 / 114 |
| OFTEN | metAndReturned | +1.46 [1.25, 1.67] | 1 | 128 / 4 / 33 |

In HALF, slightly more labels favour MBVI3 than BELIEF on `total` (84 to 76). The median is −1, yet the mean favours
BELIEF by 52.6 steps. BELIEF's HALF gain therefore comes from fewer, larger differences; G1, which sums the three
families, is CONSISTENT. In OFTEN, the after-reopening difference favours BELIEF in 124 labels and MBVI3 in 22.

**Each arm's mean per run:**

| Family | Arm | total | blocked | afterReversed | afterPermanent | futileRetests |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| RARE | BELIEF | 1,497.8 | 780.4 | 60.2 | 657.2 | 15.3 |
| RARE | MBVI3 | 1,488.0 | 769.4 | 61.9 | 656.7 | 12.2 |
| HALF | BELIEF | 1,339.8 | 707.6 | 308.6 | 323.6 | 9.0 |
| HALF | MBVI3 | 1,392.4 | 716.9 | 341.3 | 334.1 | 6.2 |
| OFTEN | BELIEF | 1,183.8 | 632.5 | 493.4 | 57.9 | 2.6 |
| OFTEN | MBVI3 | 1,266.4 | 647.9 | 558.5 | 60.0 | 1.5 |

As a share of MBVI3's steps, the differences are:

| Difference | Share of MBVI3's steps |
|---|---|
| The mix (G1) | 3.0% |
| OFTEN, total | 6.5% |
| OFTEN, after a reopening | 11.7% |
| HALF, total | 3.8% |
| RARE, BELIEF's extra cost | 0.66% |

**BELIEF's archives per run** (an archive is the belief's record that an exit changed). Each run has six blocks.
Reopenings per run: 0.52 in RARE, 3.05 in HALF, 5.43 in OFTEN.

| Family | Opened on a genuine change | of which reversing / permanent | Opened falsely | Reversal confirmed genuine / false |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| RARE | 5.08 | 0.40 / 4.68 | 1.21 | 0.16 / 1.10 |
| HALF | 4.48 | 2.11 / 2.38 | 1.23 | 1.19 / 1.05 |
| OFTEN | 3.48 | 3.11 / 0.38 | 1.02 | 1.81 / 0.84 |

BELIEF opens about one false archive a run and confirms about one false reversal a run, in every family.

## 4. From belief to behaviour: returns, measured the same way for both agents

These counts come from the environment's own step log, by [measures.mjs](measures.mjs), for every arm. They cover
reversing events, per run. "Met" means the agent tried the edge while it was blocked. "Returned" means it moved across
the edge after the reopening: in the event's own after-window, or only later.

| Family | Arm | Met | Met and returned (G4's count) | Returned in own window | Returned only later | Median steps, reopening to first return |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| RARE | BELIEF | 0.41 | 0.18 | 0.10 | 0.12 | 65 |
| RARE | MBVI3 | 0.38 | 0.09 | 0.02 | 0.11 | 228 |
| HALF | BELIEF | 2.19 | 1.25 | 0.81 | 0.78 | 73 |
| HALF | MBVI3 | 1.85 | 0.42 | 0.07 | 0.64 | 189 |
| OFTEN | BELIEF | 3.32 | 1.95 | 1.30 | 1.22 | 66 |
| OFTEN | MBVI3 | 2.31 | 0.49 | 0.09 | 0.88 | 215.5 |

In OFTEN, of the reopened edges it had met:
- **BELIEF** went back to 1.95 a run.
- **MBVI3** went back to 0.49 a run. When it did return, it was about three times slower (median 215.5 steps to 66).

Inside the event's own window, MBVI3 almost never returned (0.09 a run, against BELIEF's 1.30).

M12 could see MBVI3's returns only in 18 traces. Here they are measured in all 990 core runs.

## 5. Attribution (diagnostic arms; secondary, never in a line or the summary)

The diagnostic arms are BELIEF with exactly its parameters, archiving only genuine changes (REALONLY) or only false
alarms (FALSEONLY), by ground truth.

| Contrast | Mean [95% interval] | Median | + / − / 0 | Holds |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| A1 genuine, OFTEN, total: MBVI3 − REALONLY | +66.24 [44.55, 88.86] | 6 | 89 / 59 / 17 | yes |
| A2 genuine, OFTEN, after reopenings | +57.36 [43.84, 71.68] | 13 | 116 / 24 / 25 | yes |
| A3 false alarms, OFTEN, total: MBVI3 − FALSEONLY | +6.70 [−1.00, 15.76] | 0 | 29 / 11 / 125 | **no** |
| A4 genuine, mix | +89.28 [56.95, 123.62] | 15 | 92 / 72 / 1 | yes |
| A5 false alarms, mix | +20.05 [9.32, 34.44] | 0 | 56 / 15 / 94 | yes |

**The split is exact:** in every label and family, REALONLY opened no false archive and FALSEONLY no genuine one.

| Scope | Label |
|---|---|
| OFTEN (`gainSource`) | **GENUINE REVERSALS** |
| Mix (`gainSourceMix`) | **GENUINE REVERSALS AND FALSE-ALARM REPAIR** |

In OFTEN, the gain needs the belief about genuine changes; archiving false alarms alone gives no gain that the
interval can separate from zero. Over the mix, false-alarm archiving alone also helps, by about 20 steps a label.
It is spread over the three families: FALSEONLY's mean totals are 5.9 (RARE), 7.4 (HALF) and 6.7 (OFTEN) steps below
MBVI3's.

**Limits, as in the spec:**
- The two diagnostic effects need not add up to BELIEF's.
- The diagnostic arms intervene on what the belief archives. They do not show that returning causes the gain.

## 6. Moderator: after-window difference by the block's detour excess (descriptive, pre-declared)

For each reversing event, the event's after-window steps are compared, MBVI3 − BELIEF. Events are grouped by the block's
detour excess: how many steps longer the shortest start–goal route becomes when that edge is cut, given the
permanent cuts before it.

| Family | Excess 0: events, mean difference | Excess 1–3 | Excess 4+ |
|---|---|---|---|
| RARE | 24, +1.2 | 58, +2.8 | 3, +34.7 |
| HALF | 178, +4.6 | 271, +8.5 | 55, +41.3 |
| OFTEN | 296, +8.5 | 479, +9.4 | 121, +30.9 |

The difference rises with the detour excess in every family. Most of the rise comes in the 4+ group (RARE has only 3
such events). This is the direction the proposal expected if M12's account carries over. It is still a description,
not a test of that account.

The difference is also positive where the block forced no detour at all (excess 0). That part cannot come from
regaining a shorter route through that edge.

## 7. Beside CF-06 and M11

| Effect | CF-06 (development family) | M11 (app graph) | M13 (modular family) |
|---|---|---|---|
| Net advantage, standardized (G1 mean / SD) | 0.444 | 0.498 | **0.562** |
| Recovery after a reopening, OFTEN (steps a run) | +43.5 | +11.1 | **+65.1** |
| Cost in RARE (steps a run) | +17.4 | +2.9 | **+9.8** |

All effects point the same way as on the two earlier families. The standardized net effect is the largest of the
three.

Step counts are not on one scale across studies. M13's runs are about twice as long as M11's: OFTEN MBVI3 takes 1,266
steps a run here and 610 there. So the raw recovery and cost figures compare only roughly.

The structural record (SPEC.md) shows how the modular family differs from the development family:
- clustering 0.355 against 0.053;
- cyclomatic number 13 against 7;
- 10% of blocks costing a detour of 4+ steps, against 3%.

The study cannot say which of these matters.

## 8. Where the recovery difference comes from (descriptive, written after the result)

This section is not part of the reading. It repeats M11's §4 split on this run, so that M13 can be read beside M11 and
M12. The script is [results/check/crossing_split.mjs](results/check/crossing_split.mjs), adapted from M11's, with its
output in [results/check/crossing_split.out](results/check/crossing_split.out).

Labels are grouped by which agent crossed a reopened edge at least once.

**In the edge's own after-window (M11's definition):**

| Family | Reopenings | BELIEF crossed | MBVI3 crossed |
|---|---|---|---|
| RARE | 85 | 17 (20.0%) | 4 (4.7%) |
| HALF | 504 | 134 (26.6%) | 12 (2.4%) |
| OFTEN | 896 | 215 (24.0%) | 14 (1.6%) |

**The OFTEN recovery difference** (`afterReversed`, MBVI3 − BELIEF, 10,738 steps summed over 165 labels), split by who
crossed in the own window:

| Who crossed a reopened edge | Labels | Share of the summed difference |
|---|---|---|
| Neither agent | 36 | +150 (1.4%) |
| BELIEF only | 117 | +10,419 (97.0%) |
| MBVI3 only | 1 | −20 (−0.2%) |
| Both | 11 | +189 (1.8%) |

**This is the reverse of M11.** On the app graph, 61% of the OFTEN recovery difference came from labels where neither
agent crossed a reopened edge, and BELIEF crossed on 4.6% of reopenings.

On the modular family:
- BELIEF re-crosses on about a quarter of reopenings in its own window, against MBVI3's 1.6%.
- Almost all of the recovery difference sits in labels where BELIEF re-used a reopened route.

With M11's own definition, "recovers faster after a reopening" here largely is "goes back to the reopened route
sooner". This is observational: it groups labels by what happened and does not show that the crossing causes the
saving.

The same split by "returned at any time" is also in the output. It moves labels from "BELIEF only" to "both", because
MBVI3 does return later in many labels (on 17.7% of OFTEN reopenings, against BELIEF's 46.5%). By that grouping, labels where only BELIEF returned carry 60.8% of the difference, and
labels where both returned carry 39.4%.

## 9. The traces

The spec fixed the traces in advance: labels 0, 1 and 2, every family, BELIEF and MBVI3.

**The pre-declared illustration** ([results/check/verify_m13.out](results/check/verify_m13.out)). The rule was the
first reversing event that BELIEF met, in `m13|modular|0` OFTEN. It picked event 1, on the edge between nodes 20
and 23.

**Neither agent returned to that edge, at any time.** What the trace shows is the beliefs diverging at an edge
neither went back to:

| Moment | BELIEF's predicted chance the exit 23 → 20 works | MBVI3's |
|---|---|---|
| First blocked try, t = 790 | 0.85 | 0.85 |
| Just before the reopening | 0.30 | 0.15 |
| At the reopening, t = 855 | 0.30 | 0.15 |

Both agents took the same actions until t = 1,133, so they met the block identically. At the reopening, BELIEF held twice MBVI3's estimate,
but neither tried the exit again. In the same run:
- BELIEF met three reopened edges and returned to one, 654 steps after its reopening;
- MBVI3 met two and returned to none.

**All 18 traces, described** ([results/check/trace_summary.mjs](results/check/trace_summary.mjs),
[results/check/trace_summary.out](results/check/trace_summary.out); written after the result; every trace listed):
- **The traced RARE runs contain no reopening:** all their blocks are permanent.
- **Returns:**
  - BELIEF returned to a reopened edge in all six HALF and OFTEN pairs: 16 reopened edges in all, 9 of them in the
    event's own window.
  - MBVI3 returned to 3, all after the own window: one in label 2 HALF and two in label 2 OFTEN.
- **Reopenings followed in both traces:** BELIEF's predicted chance was higher at 14 and lower at 1. Its highest value
  at a reopening was 0.51, and MBVI3's was 0.22.
- **Label 1 OFTEN, the largest traced difference:** BELIEF took 1,237 steps to MBVI3's 1,734.
  - BELIEF returned on all six reopenings, five of them in the own window.
  - MBVI3 met the first block and never came back. Its actions in label 1 OFTEN and label 1 HALF are identical for the
    whole run (2,457 steps each, every move and every failure the same), although the two schedules differ from the
    second block on. So it never tried an edge at a moment when the two schedules disagreed about it.
- **Cost:** BELIEF tried blocked edges more often than MBVI3 in every traced pair (for example 63 to 9 in label 1
  HALF). In RARE this is its cost: 1,178 to 1,156, 2,187 to 2,125 and 1,672 to 1,660 steps.

## 10. Disclosures

- **Looks before the freeze,** as listed in the spec:
  - the structure survey (no agent);
  - the structural record (no agent);
  - the power calculation (stored CF-06 and M11 rows and the frozen bank; no agent);
  - the regression tests, on development-family worlds;
  - two driver smoke runs on development-family worlds, checked for structure, determinism and timing only.

  No agent ran on a modular world before this run.
- **Written after the result and not part of the reading:**
  - §8 and the trace descriptions in §9, with their two scripts;
  - the comparison with CF-06 and M11 in §7;
  - the percentages.

  The pre-declared illustration is reported as it came out.
- **Nothing was tuned or rerun.** The `m13|modular|*` labels and the graph bank are now spent.
- **Held-out by structure, not by builder:** the agents, the task and the parameters are those developed on the
  development family. Only the worlds are new.

## 11. Limitations

1. **One family:** 165 graphs from one generator (4 modules of 6 nodes, ring bridges, degree at most 4), all with 24
   nodes and 36 edges. Other families, and larger or sparser worlds, are untested.
2. **Descriptors move together:** clustering, cycles, route length and detour length all change between the families.
   The study cannot say which one matters.
3. **Cost is real:** BELIEF loses in RARE in 133 of 165 labels. It is within the margin, but it is not zero.
4. **Observational mechanism:** G4 and §8 show that BELIEF goes back to reopened edges far more often, and that the
   recovery difference sits where it does. Neither shows that going back causes the saving. The diagnostic arms
   change what the belief archives, not whether the agent returns.
5. **Not confirmatory:** one run, by the designer, on local commits, with secondary criteria uncorrected for
   multiplicity.

## 12. Consequence

None for AD-13 or the agent, per the spec. The record may say that the adopted belief kept its advantage over MBVI3 on
one held-out structural family, in one development-level run. It also recovered reopened routes faster there, and
went back to them more often. It may not say that the belief generalizes in general.
````

## Appendix J. Descriptive analyses written after the result (RESULTS §8–§9)

### experiments/m13_generalization/results/check/crossing_split.mjs

File: `experiments/m13_generalization/results/check/crossing_split.mjs` · SHA-256 (content) `e6ab4d4d69737ec944d637bbc86277fd9c8272ab3b36dcce7f15e2fbb31dc066` · 2641 bytes · 31 lines

````js
// Descriptive split, written after the result, adapted from experiments/m11_routes/results/check/crossing_split.mjs
// so that M13 can be read beside M11 and M12. Per family, labels are grouped by which core arm crossed a reopened edge
// at least once: first in the edge's own after-window (task E5's `crossed`, M11's definition), then at any time after
// the reopening (M13's `returned`). For each group: the summed MBVI3 - BELIEF difference in afterReversed and in total
// steps. Descriptive only; not part of the reading.
// Usage: node crossing_split.mjs <path to m13_result.json>
import fs from 'node:fs';
const j = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(process.argv[2]));
const own = (r) => r.events.reduce((s, e) => s + (e.reverses ? e.crossed : 0), 0);
const any = (r) => r.m13.events.filter((e) => e.reverses && e.returned).length;
for (const [name, cr, evCrossed] of [
  ['own after-window (M11 definition)', own, (r, i) => r.events[i].crossed > 0],
  ['any time after the reopening (M13 returned)', any, (r, i) => !!r.m13.events.find((e) => e.k === i)?.returned],
]) {
  console.log(`== crossed in the ${name}`);
  for (const f of ['RARE', 'HALF', 'OFTEN']) {
    const B = new Map(), M = new Map();
    for (const r of j.rows) if (r.family === f && (r.arm === 'BELIEF' || r.arm === 'MBVI3')) (r.arm === 'BELIEF' ? B : M).set(r.world, r);
    const groups = { neither: [], beliefOnly: [], mbvi3Only: [], both: [] };
    for (const [w, b] of B) {
      const m = M.get(w);
      const g = cr(b) > 0 && cr(m) > 0 ? 'both' : cr(b) > 0 ? 'beliefOnly' : cr(m) > 0 ? 'mbvi3Only' : 'neither';
      groups[g].push({ ar: m.windows.afterReversed - b.windows.afterReversed, tot: m.metric - b.metric, tie: m.metric === b.metric });
    }
    let reversing = 0, crossedB = 0, crossedM = 0;
    for (const [w, b] of B) b.events.forEach((e, i) => { if (e.reverses) { reversing++; if (evCrossed(b, i)) crossedB++; if (evCrossed(M.get(w), i)) crossedM++; } });
    const sumAr = [...Object.values(groups)].flat().reduce((s, x) => s + x.ar, 0);
    console.log(`${f}: reversing events ${reversing}; crossed at least once BELIEF ${crossedB} (${(100 * crossedB / reversing).toFixed(1)}%), MBVI3 ${crossedM} (${(100 * crossedM / reversing).toFixed(1)}%); summed afterReversed difference ${sumAr}`);
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(groups)) { const s = v.reduce((a, x) => a + x.ar, 0); console.log(`  ${k.padEnd(10)} labels ${String(v.length).padStart(3)}; sum MBVI3-BELIEF afterReversed ${s} (${sumAr ? (100 * s / sumAr).toFixed(1) : '-'}%), total ${v.reduce((a, x) => a + x.tot, 0)}; exact ties in total ${v.filter((x) => x.tie).length}`); }
  }
}
````

````text
== crossed in the own after-window (M11 definition)
RARE: reversing events 85; crossed at least once BELIEF 17 (20.0%), MBVI3 4 (4.7%); summed afterReversed difference 293
````

### experiments/m13_generalization/results/check/trace_summary.mjs

File: `experiments/m13_generalization/results/check/trace_summary.mjs` · SHA-256 (content) `6d8ad672c6dc6243209dd63941c7b963d26c54d33aa9d8cc489665160d36e262` · 3566 bytes · 39 lines

````js
// Descriptive summary of the 18 pre-chosen traces (labels 0-2, all families, BELIEF and MBVI3), written after the
// result. Adapted from experiments/m11_routes/results/check/trace_summary.mjs. Descriptive only: no test, no
// selection; every trace is listed. Returns come from the stored M13 behaviour measures (row.m13.events).
// Usage: node trace_summary.mjs <path to m13_result.json>
import fs from 'node:fs';
const j = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(process.argv[2]));
const row = (w, f, a) => j.rows.find((r) => r.world === w && r.family === f && r.arm === a);
const reopen = { higher: 0, lower: 0, equal: 0 };
let maxB = 0, maxM = 0;
for (const w of ['m13|modular|0', 'm13|modular|1', 'm13|modular|2']) for (const f of ['RARE', 'HALF', 'OFTEN']) {
  const rb = row(w, f, 'BELIEF'), rm = row(w, f, 'MBVI3');
  const tb = j.traces.find((t) => t.world === w && t.family === f && t.arm === 'BELIEF');
  const tm = j.traces.find((t) => t.world === w && t.family === f && t.arm === 'MBVI3');
  const sameActions = tb.steps.length === tm.steps.length && tb.steps.every((s, i) => s[1] === tm.steps[i][1] && s[2] === tm.steps[i][2]);
  const firstDiff = sameActions ? null : tb.steps.findIndex((s, i) => !tm.steps[i] || s[1] !== tm.steps[i][1] || s[2] !== tm.steps[i][2]);
  const blockedTries = (t) => t.steps.filter((s) => s[4] === 1).length;
  const ret = (r) => r.m13.events.filter((e) => e.reverses).map((e) => (e.returned ? (e.crossedOwn > 0 ? 'O' : 'L') : e.met ? 'm' : '.')).join('');
  console.log(`${w} ${f}: total B ${rb.metric} M ${rm.metric}; reverses ${rb.events.map((e) => (e.reverses ? 'R' : 'P')).join('')}; reversing events (O returned in own window, L only later, m met and not returned, . neither) B ${ret(rb) || '-'} M ${ret(rm) || '-'}; failed tries on blocked edges B ${blockedTries(tb)} M ${blockedTries(tm)}; same actions ${sameActions}${firstDiff !== null ? ` (first difference at step index ${firstDiff}, t=${tb.steps[firstDiff]?.[0]})` : ''}; B archives ${tb.beliefEvents.filter((e) => e.type === 'open').length} opened, ${tb.beliefEvents.filter((e) => e.type !== 'open').length} other`);
  // for each followed exit, p at each reopening (first point after a blocked stretch) for both arms
  const ends = (pts) => pts.filter((p, i) => i > 0 && pts[i - 1][2] === 1 && p[2] === 0).map((p) => [p[0], p[1]]);
  for (const fb of tb.followed) {
    const fm = tm.followed.find((x) => x.from === fb.from && x.to === fb.to);
    const eb = ends(fb.points), em = fm ? ends(fm.points) : [];
    // the i-th reopening of the same exit in each trace is the same scheduled event (times differ once the runs diverge)
    eb.forEach(([, p], i) => {
      maxB = Math.max(maxB, p);
      const m = em[i];
      if (m) { reopen[p > m[1] ? 'higher' : p < m[1] ? 'lower' : 'equal']++; }
    });
    for (const [, p] of em) maxM = Math.max(maxM, p);
    console.log(`    ${fb.from}->${fb.to}: p at each reopening B [${eb.map(([t, p]) => `t${t}:${p}`).join(' ')}] M [${fm ? em.map(([t, p]) => `t${t}:${p}`).join(' ') : 'not followed'}]`);
  }
  for (const fm of tm.followed) if (!tb.followed.some((x) => x.from === fm.from && x.to === fm.to)) {
    const em = ends(fm.points);
    for (const [, p] of em) maxM = Math.max(maxM, p);
    console.log(`    ${fm.from}->${fm.to}: followed for M only, p at each reopening M [${em.map(([t, p]) => `t${t}:${p}`).join(' ')}]`);
  }
}
console.log(`\nreopenings followed in both arms' traces: BELIEF's p higher ${reopen.higher}, lower ${reopen.lower}, equal ${reopen.equal}; highest p at a reopening B ${maxB}, M ${maxM}`);
````
