## Appendix K. Review-package supplements, written after the result

### experiments/m13_generalization/review/check_measures_from_traces.mjs

File: `experiments/m13_generalization/review/check_measures_from_traces.mjs` · SHA-256 (content) `8b83ef5ce247e95cc64c0be58dbc2753f914cd4020d6cddd7cb157d130b396f9` · 4167 bytes · 52 lines

````js
// Supplementary audit check for the M13 review package, written 2026-11-18 AFTER the result (not part of the
// reading, and not the pre-result independent check). It addresses one auditability gap: G4's inputs (`met` and
// `crossedLater`) are computed by measures.mjs from the environment's own step log (GraphEnv.trace()), which the result
// file does not store. The 18 traced runs do store a second, separate step log: the passive recorder's
// (experiments/m11_routes/trace.mjs, one entry per observe, before the agent updates, with the task's ground truth for
// the exit tried). This script rebuilds every reversing event's measures from that recorder log with its own code and
// compares them with the stored row.m13.events. It also checks that the ground truth the recorder saw agrees with the
// reconstructed event windows (blocked while the event's edge is blocked, open in its own after-window).
// It imports nothing from the repository.
// Usage: node check_measures_from_traces.mjs <path to m13_result.json>
import fs from 'node:fs';
const j = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(process.argv[2]));
const key = (u, v) => (u < v ? `${u}-${v}` : `${v}-${u}`);
let bad = 0, events = 0, compared = 0, groundTruthChecks = 0;
const fail = (msg) => { bad++; console.log(`FAIL  ${msg}`); };
for (const t of j.traces) {
  const row = j.rows.find((r) => r.world === t.world && r.family === t.family && r.arm === t.arm);
  const S = t.steps; // [t, from, to, moved, blocked, pWorks]
  if (!S.every((s, i) => s[0] === i)) fail(`${t.world} ${t.family} ${t.arm}: recorder steps are not consecutive`);
  let at = S.length - row.metric;
  const mine = [];
  t.schedule.forEach((e, k) => {
    if (e.skipped) return;
    const ev = row.events[k], blockAt = at, afterAt = at + ev.blocked, endAt = afterAt + ev.after, K = key(...e.edge);
    at = endAt;
    let met = false, attemptsOwn = 0, crossedOwn = 0, crossedLater = 0, first = null;
    for (let i = blockAt; i < S.length; i++) {
      const s = S[i];
      if (key(s[1], s[2]) !== K) continue;
      if (i < afterAt) { met = true; groundTruthChecks++; if (s[4] !== 1 || s[3] !== 0) fail(`${t.world} ${t.family} ${t.arm} event ${k}: a try at t=${i} in the blocked window is not recorded as blocked and failed`); continue; }
      if (i < endAt) { attemptsOwn++; if (s[3]) crossedOwn++; if (e.reverses) { groundTruthChecks++; if (s[4] !== 0) fail(`${t.world} ${t.family} ${t.arm} event ${k}: a try at t=${i} in the own after-window of a reversing event is recorded as blocked`); } }
      else if (s[3]) crossedLater++;
      if (s[3] && first === null) first = i;
    }
    mine.push({ k, reverses: e.reverses, met, attemptsOwn, crossedOwn, crossedLater, returned: e.reverses && crossedOwn + crossedLater > 0, delay: e.reverses && first !== null ? first - afterAt : null });
  });
  if (at !== S.length) fail(`${t.world} ${t.family} ${t.arm}: event windows end at ${at}, the recorder log has ${S.length} steps`);
  const stored = row.m13.events;
  if (stored.length !== mine.length) fail(`${t.world} ${t.family} ${t.arm}: ${stored.length} stored events, ${mine.length} rebuilt`);
  for (const m of mine) {
    events++;
    const s = stored.find((x) => x.k === m.k);
    compared++;
    if (!s || JSON.stringify(s) !== JSON.stringify(m)) fail(`${t.world} ${t.family} ${t.arm} event ${m.k}: stored ${JSON.stringify(s)} rebuilt ${JSON.stringify(m)}`);
  }
  const rev = mine.filter((e) => e.reverses);
  const g4 = rev.filter((e) => e.met && e.returned).length;
  if (g4 !== row.m13.metAndReturned) fail(`${t.world} ${t.family} ${t.arm}: metAndReturned stored ${row.m13.metAndReturned}, rebuilt ${g4}`);
  console.log(`${t.world} ${t.family} ${t.arm}: ${S.length} recorder steps; ${mine.length} live events (${rev.length} reversing); met ${rev.filter((e) => e.met).length}, met and returned ${g4} (stored ${row.m13.metAndReturned})`);
}
console.log(`\n${j.traces.length} traces, ${events} live events rebuilt from the recorder log, ${compared} compared field by field with row.m13.events, ${groundTruthChecks} ground-truth window checks; ${bad === 0 ? 'all agree' : `${bad} FAILURES`}`);
process.exit(bad === 0 ? 0 : 1);
````

### experiments/m13_generalization/review/per_label.mjs

File: `experiments/m13_generalization/review/per_label.mjs` · SHA-256 (content) `46fae3eb9deb079e861cd1a469816ead8c2dcadd21f64c3a39c0c145f1b6a19e` · 2614 bytes · 34 lines

````js
// Per-label extract for the M13 review package, written 2026-11-18 after the result. It changes nothing: it reads
// results/m13_result.json and writes one CSV row per label with the step counts each criterion and attribution contrast
// is built from, and the contrasts themselves. A reviewer can recompute every mean, median and sign count in
// RESULTS.md from this file alone, and every bootstrap interval with cognitive/testbed/stats.mjs (pairedBootstrap,
// seeded by the tags listed in REVIEW_PACKAGE.md). It imports nothing from the repository.
// Usage: node per_label.mjs <path to m13_result.json>  > per_label.csv
import fs from 'node:fs';
const j = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(process.argv[2]));
const F = ['RARE', 'HALF', 'OFTEN'], ARMS = ['BELIEF', 'MBVI3', 'BELIEF_REALONLY', 'BELIEF_FALSEONLY'];
const short = { BELIEF: 'B', MBVI3: 'M', BELIEF_REALONLY: 'R', BELIEF_FALSEONLY: 'F' };
const cell = new Map(j.rows.map((r) => [`${r.world}|${r.family}|${r.arm}`, r]));
const get = (w, f, a) => cell.get(`${w}|${f}|${a}`);
const labels = [...new Set(j.rows.map((r) => r.world))].sort((a, b) => Number(a.split('|')[2]) - Number(b.split('|')[2]));
const cols = ['label'];
for (const f of F) for (const a of ARMS) cols.push(`total_${f}_${short[a]}`);
for (const a of ARMS) cols.push(`afterReversed_OFTEN_${short[a]}`);
for (const a of ['BELIEF', 'MBVI3']) cols.push(`metAndReturned_OFTEN_${short[a]}`);
cols.push('G1_mix_M_minus_B', 'G2_RARE_B_minus_M', 'G3_OFTEN_after_M_minus_B', 'G4_OFTEN_metAndReturned_B_minus_M',
  'A1_OFTEN_M_minus_R', 'A2_OFTEN_after_M_minus_R', 'A3_OFTEN_M_minus_F', 'A4_mix_M_minus_R', 'A5_mix_M_minus_F');
const out = [cols.join(',')];
for (const w of labels) {
  const v = [w];
  for (const f of F) for (const a of ARMS) v.push(get(w, f, a).metric);
  for (const a of ARMS) v.push(get(w, 'OFTEN', a).windows.afterReversed);
  for (const a of ['BELIEF', 'MBVI3']) v.push(get(w, 'OFTEN', a).m13.metAndReturned);
  const tot = (f, a) => get(w, f, a).metric, mix = (a) => F.reduce((s, f) => s + tot(f, 'MBVI3') - tot(f, a), 0);
  const after = (a) => get(w, 'OFTEN', a).windows.afterReversed;
  v.push(mix('BELIEF'), tot('RARE', 'BELIEF') - tot('RARE', 'MBVI3'), after('MBVI3') - after('BELIEF'),
    get(w, 'OFTEN', 'BELIEF').m13.metAndReturned - get(w, 'OFTEN', 'MBVI3').m13.metAndReturned,
    tot('OFTEN', 'MBVI3') - tot('OFTEN', 'BELIEF_REALONLY'), after('MBVI3') - after('BELIEF_REALONLY'),
    tot('OFTEN', 'MBVI3') - tot('OFTEN', 'BELIEF_FALSEONLY'), mix('BELIEF_REALONLY'), mix('BELIEF_FALSEONLY'));
  out.push(v.join(','));
}
process.stdout.write(out.join('\n') + '\n');
````

### experiments/m13_generalization/review/build_bundle.mjs

File: `experiments/m13_generalization/review/build_bundle.mjs` · SHA-256 (content) `fe525e56647b209efadc03318cbe21dfd716170ebbe320001412c69afc70863f` · 9047 bytes · 91 lines

`````js
// Builds the single-file M13 review bundle: REVIEW_PACKAGE.md (the cover), then every file of the package verbatim
// with its SHA-256. Written 2026-11-18 for the review package; it reads files and git metadata only and changes nothing
// else. Run from the repository root:  node experiments/m13_generalization/review/build_bundle.mjs
// Hashes: "raw" is the SHA-256 of the bytes on disk; "content" reads CRLF as LF (the PINS.json convention, because
// cognitive/ has no exact-bytes rule and a checkout may convert line endings). Files are reproduced with LF endings.
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';

const M = 'experiments/m13_generalization';
const OUT = `${M}/review/M13_REVIEW_BUNDLE.md`;
const sha = (b) => crypto.createHash('sha256').update(b).digest('hex');
const git = (...a) => execFileSync('git', a, { encoding: 'utf8', maxBuffer: 1 << 26 }).replace(/\r\n/g, '\n');
const lang = (p) => (p.endsWith('.md') ? 'markdown' : p.endsWith('.json') ? 'json' : p.endsWith('.mjs') ? 'js' : p.endsWith('.ps1') ? 'powershell' : p.endsWith('.csv') ? 'csv' : 'text');
const fenceFor = (text) => '`'.repeat(Math.max(4, ...[...text.matchAll(/`+/g)].map((m) => m[0].length + 1)));

const pins = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(`${M}/PINS.json`, 'utf8')).files;
const pinned = Object.keys(pins);
const implementation = ['worlds_modular.mjs', 'structure.mjs', 'make_bank.mjs', 'measures.mjs', 'm13_lib.mjs', 'pins.mjs', 'run_m13.mjs', 'structure.json'].map((f) => `${M}/${f}`);
const bank = `${M}/graph_bank.json`;
const dependencies = pinned.filter((p) => !implementation.includes(p) && p !== bank).sort();

const APPENDICES = [
  ['A', 'Frozen specification and its recorded hashes', [`${M}/SPEC.md`, `${M}/SPEC.sha256`, `${M}/PINS.json`]],
  ['B', 'The approved proposal and its no-agent inputs', [`${M}/PROPOSAL.md`, `${M}/proposal_inputs/structure_survey.mjs`, `${M}/proposal_inputs/structure_survey.out`, `${M}/proposal_inputs/power_inputs.mjs`, `${M}/proposal_inputs/power_inputs.out`]],
  ['C', 'M13 implementation (generator, structure, measures, library, pins, driver)', implementation],
  ['D', 'All other pinned dependencies (agents, models, planner, task, environment, statistics, recorder, test runner)', dependencies],
  ['E', 'Regression tests for M13 and the adopted agent (run by the driver through scripts/test.mjs; not pinned)', ['cognitive/tests/test_m13_generalization.mjs', 'cognitive/tests/test_adopted_agent.mjs']],
  ['F', 'Sample size and power (no agent)', [`${M}/power/power_m13.mjs`, `${M}/power/power_m13.out`, `${M}/power/power_m13.json`]],
  ['G', 'Run provenance', [`${M}/results/run/run_m13_awake.ps1`, `${M}/results/run/hold_awake_until.ps1`, `${M}/results/run/m13_run.log`, `${M}/results/run/m13_run.stdout`, `${M}/results/run/m13_run.stderr`, `${M}/results/run/m13_power_events.txt`, `${M}/results/SHA256SUMS`]],
  ['H', 'Independent check, written before the result', [`${M}/results/check/verify_m13.mjs`, `${M}/results/check/verify_m13.out`]],
  ['I', 'The results record', [`${M}/RESULTS.md`]],
  ['J', 'Descriptive analyses written after the result (RESULTS §8–§9)', [`${M}/results/check/crossing_split.mjs`, `${M}/results/check/crossing_split.out`, `${M}/results/check/trace_summary.mjs`, `${M}/results/check/trace_summary.out`]],
  ['K', 'Review-package supplements, written after the result', [`${M}/review/check_measures_from_traces.mjs`, `${M}/review/check_measures_from_traces.out`, `${M}/review/per_label.mjs`, `${M}/review/per_label.csv`, `${M}/review/build_bundle.mjs`]],
  ['N', 'The frozen graph bank (pinned data; regenerated and compared by make_bank.mjs --check)', [bank]],
];

const files = APPENDICES.flatMap(([, , fs_]) => fs_);
const missing = files.filter((f) => !fs.existsSync(f));
if (missing.length) throw new Error(`missing: ${missing.join(', ')}`);
// Files under experiments/m13_generalization are stored byte-exact (-text), so their raw bytes are the same in every
// checkout; elsewhere only the content (CRLF read as LF) is checkout-independent, so only it is reported.
const info = (p) => {
  const raw = fs.readFileSync(p), text = raw.toString('utf8').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n'), exact = p.startsWith(`${M}/`);
  const content = sha(Buffer.from(text, 'utf8'));
  return { p, bytes: exact ? raw.length : Buffer.byteLength(text, 'utf8'), raw: exact ? sha(raw) : content, content, text, lines: text.split('\n').length - (text.endsWith('\n') ? 1 : 0) };
};
const I = new Map([`${M}/review/REVIEW_PACKAGE.md`, ...files].map((p) => [p, info(p)]));
for (const p of pinned) if (pins[p] !== I.get(p).content) throw new Error(`${p} does not match PINS.json`);

const parts = [];
parts.push(I.get(`${M}/review/REVIEW_PACKAGE.md`).text.trimEnd(), '', '---', '', '# Bundle contents');
parts.push('', `Generated by \`${M}/review/build_bundle.mjs\` from the results commit \`b9a05e0\` plus the review folder added on top of it. Rebuilding it at the package commit reproduces the same content.`);
parts.push('Every file below is reproduced verbatim, with LF line endings. "content" is its SHA-256 with CRLF read as LF; for every pinned file it equals `PINS.json`. "raw" is the SHA-256 of the stored bytes, shown only where it differs: the two run files PowerShell wrote with CRLF endings, whose raw hash is the one in `results/SHA256SUMS`. Files under `experiments/m13_generalization/` are stored byte-exact; elsewhere a checkout may convert line endings, so only the content hash is given. "Bytes" is the stored size for files under `experiments/m13_generalization/`, and the LF content size elsewhere.');
parts.push('', '| Appendix | File | Bytes | Lines | SHA-256 (content) |', '|---|---|---|---|---|');
{ const x = I.get(`${M}/review/REVIEW_PACKAGE.md`); parts.push(`| cover | \`${x.p}\` | ${x.bytes} | ${x.lines} | \`${x.content}\` |`); }
for (const [L, , fs_] of APPENDICES) for (const p of fs_) { const x = I.get(p); parts.push(`| ${L} | \`${p}\` | ${x.bytes} | ${x.lines} | \`${x.content}\`${x.raw !== x.content ? ` (raw \`${x.raw}\`)` : ''} |`); }
parts.push('', 'Generated sections, not files: G2 (the M13 commit history), G3 (the raw result without `rows` and `traces`, and one example row), L (the PROJECT_STATE changes).');

const section = (p, title) => {
  const x = I.get(p), fence = fenceFor(x.text);
  return [``, `### ${title ?? p}`, ``, `File: \`${p}\` · SHA-256 (content) \`${x.content}\`${x.raw !== x.content ? ` · raw \`${x.raw}\`` : ''} · ${x.bytes} bytes · ${x.lines} lines`, ``, `${fence}${lang(p)}`, x.text.endsWith('\n') ? x.text.slice(0, -1) : x.text, fence].join('\n');
};

for (const [L, title, fs_] of APPENDICES) {
  parts.push('', '---', '', `## Appendix ${L}. ${title}`);
  if (L === 'D') parts.push('', `${fs_.length} files. Together with appendix C, the graph bank (N) and the two scripts the driver runs, they are the ${pinned.length} files of \`PINS.json\`.`);
  for (const p of fs_) parts.push(section(p));
  if (L === 'G') {
    const log = git('log', '--format=%H %ad %s', '--date=iso-strict', '728dd71..b9a05e0');
    const stats = ['6381450', 'b48453e', '8619d71', 'b9a05e0'].map((c) => git('show', '--stat=160', '--format=commit %H%n%s%n', c)).join('\n');
    const g2 = `${log}\n${stats}`;
    parts.push('', '### G2. The M13 commit history (generated: `git log 728dd71..b9a05e0` and `git show --stat` of each commit)', '', `${fenceFor(g2)}text`, g2.trimEnd(), fenceFor(g2));
    const resultPath = `${M}/results/m13_result.json`, raw = fs.readFileSync(resultPath), j = JSON.parse(raw);
    const { rows, traces, ...rest } = j;
    const example = rows.find((r) => r.world === `m13|modular|0` && r.family === 'OFTEN' && r.arm === 'BELIEF');
    const g3 = JSON.stringify(rest, null, 1);
    parts.push('', `### G3. The raw result, every top-level field except \`rows\` and \`traces\` (generated)`, '',
      `File: \`${resultPath}\` · SHA-256 \`${sha(raw)}\` · ${raw.length} bytes. It holds ${rows.length} rows (run A: 165 labels × 3 families × 4 arms) and ${traces.length} traces (labels 0–2, all families, BELIEF and MBVI3), which are too large to reproduce here; \`review/per_label.csv\` (appendix K) holds every per-label number the criteria and the attribution use. Field order and values below are exactly the file's.`,
      '', `${fenceFor(g3)}json`, g3, fenceFor(g3),
      '', 'One row, verbatim (`m13|modular|0`, OFTEN, BELIEF), to show the row structure:', '', '````json', JSON.stringify(example, null, 1), '````');
  }
  if (L === 'K') {
    const d = git('diff', '728dd71', 'b9a05e0', '--', 'docs/engineering/PROJECT_STATE.md');
    parts.push('', '---', '', '## Appendix L. PROJECT_STATE changes for M13 (generated: `git diff 728dd71 b9a05e0 -- docs/engineering/PROJECT_STATE.md`)', '', `${fenceFor(d)}diff`, d.trimEnd(), fenceFor(d));
  }
}
fs.writeFileSync(OUT, parts.join('\n') + '\n');
const out = fs.readFileSync(OUT);
console.log(`${OUT}: ${out.length} bytes, SHA-256 ${sha(out)}; ${files.length} files reproduced`);
`````

Unlike M11, 97% of the OFTEN recovery difference sits in labels where only BELIEF re-crossed a reopened edge in its own window (descriptive). Freeze `8619d71`, results in the commit after it |
 | 2026-11-18 | **M12** belief-to-behaviour mechanism analysis of the stored M11 rows and traces (`experiments/m12_mechanism/`); no run, no replay, no agent change | **Accepted by the owner as a useful explanation.** Observational, not causal proof. The 61% comes from BELIEF going back to exits its detector had written off: reopened edges, mostly 2–4 windows after the reopening, beyond M11's crossing window; and false-alarm repairs. In the 103 labels, the 51 with such a return carry +924 of +916; the other 52 carry −8. BELIEF's returns are observed in all 810 runs; MBVI3's non-return only in the 18 traces (0 of 8). M11 RESULTS.md amended (dated), verdict unchanged |
 | 2026-11-17 | **M11** the adopted belief (AD-13) against MBVI3 under controlled route blocking and reopening on the app's own graph (`experiments/m11_routes/`), 135 labels × 3 families, outside the app | **DEMONSTRATED ON THE MINIFLYWIRE GRAPH** (development-level). D1 recovery in OFTEN +11.1 [7.2, 15.3]; D2 cost in RARE +2.9 [0.7, 4.9] (margin 25); D3 crossings of reopened edges +0.64 [0.19, 1.14]. All smaller than CF-06. Reopened edges are rarely re-used inside their own after-window (OFTEN: 4.6% of reopenings by BELIEF, 1.2% by MBVI3). The original record said 61% of the recovery difference came from labels where no reopened edge was ever re-used, with the source unidentified; **amended 2026-11-18 after M12**: in most of those labels BELIEF went back later (see M12). Gain from genuine reversals and false-alarm repair. Adversarial review: SOUND WITH CORRECTIONS, verdict unchanged. +**M13 RECORDED (2026-11-18): NET ADVANTAGE RETAINED ON THE MODULAR FAMILY, WITHIN THE SAFETY MARGIN** — cognitive
+generalization (`experiments/m13_generalization/RESULTS.md`). Development-level: one run, by the designer, on local
+commits.
+- **The run:** frozen in `8619d71` on the owner's word "Freeze and run". Run once, 2026-11-18 06:07:14Z–08:16:28Z,
+  exit 0, at HEAD `8619d71` throughout. The tests passed 195/195 before it started.
+  - 1,980 runs in run A, 990 core runs in run B, with identical core rows.
+  - 0 exclusions; 1 skipped event of 2,970, as predicted from the bank.
+  - No standby during the run (System log extract lodged).
+  - The result file `m13_result.json` is `a0cd87d4…`. The independent check, written before the result, passes in
+    full.
+- **Lines:**
+  - Validity: VALID.
+  - Net advantage: RETAINED, +125.4 [92.0, 160.1]. The median is +44 and 102 labels favour BELIEF, 62 MBVI3. This
+    is 3.0% of MBVI3's steps.
+  - Safety: SAFE, RARE cost +9.8 [3.6, 14.9]. BELIEF costs more in 133 of 165 labels.
+  - Recovery: SHOWN, +65.1 [50.9, 79.9].
+  - Behavioural mechanism: SHOWN, +1.46 [1.25, 1.67]. The minimum detectable effect was 0.30.
+  - gainShape: CONSISTENT for both G1 and G3.
+- **Attribution (exact split):** A1, A2, A4 and A5 hold; A3, false alarms in OFTEN, does not (+6.7 [−1.0, 15.8]).
+  OFTEN reads GENUINE REVERSALS; the mix reads GENUINE REVERSALS AND FALSE-ALARM REPAIR.
+- **Returns, measured for both agents in every run:** in OFTEN, BELIEF went back to 1.95 of the reopened edges it had
+  met per run, MBVI3 to 0.49. Median time to the first return: 66 against 215.5 steps.
+- **Moderator (descriptive):** the after-window difference rises with the block's detour excess (OFTEN +8.5, +9.4,
+  +30.9 for excess 0, 1–3, 4+).
+- **Descriptive, after the result:** 97% of the OFTEN recovery difference sits in labels where only BELIEF crossed a
+  reopened edge in its own window. In M11 it was 61% in labels where neither did. The pre-declared illustration
+  (label 0 OFTEN, event 1) shows the beliefs diverging (0.30 against 0.15 at the reopening), but neither agent
+  returned to that edge.
+- **Allowed wording:** the belief kept its advantage over MBVI3 on one held-out structural family, in one
+  development-level run, and recovered reopened routes faster there. Not: "generalizes" in general.
+- **Consequence:** none for AD-13 or the agent. The `m13|modular|*` labels and the graph bank are now spent.
+
+The design, as frozen. The proposal was approved on the owner's word "Approve with diagnostics".
+- **Revision 2** makes the owner's three corrections, approved with the driver's alignment ("Approve both").
+  - **Verdict:** five independent lines (validity; net advantage G1 primary; safety G2; recovery G3 and behavioural
+    mechanism G4, both secondary) and one summary statement built from G0, G1 and G2 only. A G3 or G4 failure cannot
+    erase a retained net advantage.
+  - **Exclusions:** computed from the frozen bank, there are none, so the analysed n is fixed at 165. G0 requires
+    that nothing be excluded; the old 10% rule is withdrawn.
+  - **Power** (`power/power_m13.mjs`, reproducible): ⌈((1.959964 + 0.841621) / 0.22)²⌉ = 163 labels; power 0.807 at
+    165. Simulated with the study's own bootstrap: 0.806 with normal noise, 0.911 and 0.846 with the recorded CF-06 and
+    M11 shapes.
+  - **Attribution:** contrasts A1–A5, CF-06's rule verbatim, with an exactness check; ground-truth isolation
+    documented.
+- **Question:** does the AD-13 belief keep its advantage over MBVI3, with nothing tuned, on a held-out family of
+  worlds structurally unlike the development family?
+- **Worlds:** modular rings (4 × 6 nodes, 8 edges per module, a ring of single bridges, degree ≤ 4; 24 nodes like the
+  development family). A new graph per label: 165 graphs frozen in `graph_bank.json`, with fresh labels
+  `m13|modular|0..164`.
+- **Novelty (structure only, no agent):**
+  - clustering: the bank's 5th percentile is 0.243, against the development family's 95th percentile of 0.167;
+  - cyclomatic number 13 against 7;
+  - long-detour blocks 10% against 3%.
+- **Arms:** BELIEF (`makeAdoptedAgent`) and MBVI3, plus CF-06's diagnostic arms BELIEF_REALONLY / BELIEF_FALSEONLY
+  (secondary).
+- **Task:** E5 unchanged.
+- **Criteria** (each decided on its own; verdict lines as in revision 2 above):
+  - G0 validity: determinism, tests, novelty, no exclusion;
+  - G1 net mix advantage (primary), lower bound > 0;
+  - G2 RARE cost, upper bound < 25;
+  - G3 OFTEN recovery, lower bound > 0;
+  - G4 OFTEN reopened edges met while blocked and crossed again, BELIEF − MBVI3, lower bound > 0. Measured for both
+    agents in every run from `env.trace()`.
+- **Built:** generator, world builder (an exact copy of the testbed's hidden relabelling, proven identical), structure
+  and novelty, behaviour measures (checked in every run against the task's own counts), library, driver, and
+  `PINS.json`.
+  - The pins cover 44 files: the driver's full import closure plus data and scripts, hashed by content with CRLF read
+    as LF.
+  - The driver refuses until the spec is Frozen, every hash and pin matches, the tree is clean, the labels are fresh,
+    novelty holds and the tests pass.
+  - `cognitive/tests/test_m13_generalization.mjs` adds 11 tests; `npm test` is 195/195.
+- **Before the run, no agent ran on a modular world.** Agents ran only in the tests (`m13test|*`) and in two smoke runs
+  on development-family labels (`m13smoke|dev|*`; structure, determinism and timing checked, no measure read).
+
 **M12 RECORDED (2026-11-18): the belief-to-behaviour mechanism behind M11's recovery advantage**
 (`experiments/m12_mechanism/REPORT.md`). The owner accepted it as a useful explanation supported by the 810-run data.
 It is a post-hoc, observational analysis of the stored M11 record. Nothing was run or replayed, no agent or parameter
